# Contract API

Generated by `cargo run -p craft-ui-gallery --bin contract-api`; do not edit. Every public item of
`craft_behavior` and of the shared modules of `craft_ui` (`contract`, `layout`, `layout_components`, `primitives`,
`runtime`, `style`, `testing` and the crate root), by public path, with its signature line and the first line of
its doc comment. Inherent methods and trait methods are indented under their type. Setters written by
`#[derive(Props)]` are not listed: each `#[prop]` field of a type is a setter of the same name.

## `craft_behavior`

Pure UI behaviours (layer 1): press lifecycle, `ValueDrag`, navigation, item layouts, edit events,

  - `pub fn new(rect: ScreenRect, center: Point, zoom: f64) -> Self`. An upright, unmirrored, square-pixel view of `rect` showing `center` at `zoom`.
  - `pub const fn rotation(mut self, degrees: f64) -> Self`. The same view turned `degrees` clockwise.
  - `pub const fn flip_x(mut self, flip: bool) -> Self`. The same view mirrored left-right (or not).
  - `pub const fn aspect(mut self, aspect: f64) -> Self`. The same view with a document unit `aspect` times as wide as it is tall.

  - `pub fn affine(&self) -> Affine`. Document to screen points.
  - `pub fn to_screen(&self, p: Point) -> Pos2`. Where the view draws document point `p`.
  - `pub fn to_doc(&self, p: Pos2) -> Point`. The document point under screen position `p`.

  - `pub fn unmap_vec(&self, v: ScreenVec) -> Vec2`. The document vector a screen vector shows: the inverse of [`map_vec`](Self::map_vec).
  - `pub fn zoom_x(&self) -> f64`. Screen points per document unit along the document's x axis: `zoom × aspect`.
  - `pub fn quad(&self, r: Rect) -> [Pos2; 4]`. The screen corners of document rect `r`, in order round it: (x0, y0), (x1, y0), (x1, y1),
  - `pub fn visible(&self) -> Rect`. The document bounds of the screen rect: every document point the view can show.
  - `pub fn doc_len(&self, points: f32) -> f64`. A screen distance in document units along the document's y axis (divide by
  - `pub fn layer_transform(&self) -> Option<CanvasTransform>`. The transform a canvas body is shown through when this view only scales and moves the document: unturned,
  - `pub fn axis_aligned(&self) -> bool`. Whether the rotation is a multiple of 90°, so document axes are screen axes.

- `craft_behavior::NavOutline`: `pub trait NavOutline`. Positions, not keys: the key-free part of `DisplayOrder`.
  - `fn len(&self) -> usize`. The number of positions.
  - `fn depth(&self, at: usize) -> usize`. The nesting depth of the position, 0 for a root.
  - `fn has_children(&self, at: usize) -> bool`. Whether the position has children.
  - `fn open(&self, at: usize) -> bool`. Whether the position's children show.
  - `fn is_section(&self, at: usize) -> bool`. Whether the position is a section header, which a move skips.
  - `fn enabled(&self, at: usize) -> bool`. Whether the position can take the lead.
  - `fn row_len(&self, _row: usize) -> Option<usize>`. `RaggedGrid`: the number of positions in `row`; rows hold consecutive positions. `None` past the last row.
  - `fn columns(&self) -> usize`. `Cell`: the cells of every position (a row of a sheet).
  - `fn first_column(&self) -> Option<usize>`. `WrappedGrid`: the column of position 0 when the grid has blank cells before it (the leading days of a
  - `fn rect(&self, _at: usize) -> Option<Rect>`. `Spatial`: where the position sits, in any one coordinate space.
  - `fn is_empty(&self) -> bool`. Whether there are no positions.
- `craft_behavior::NavShape`: `pub enum NavShape { Flat, Tree, WrappedGrid { columns: usize }, BoundedGrid { columns: usize }, RaggedGrid, Cell, Spatial }`. The geometry a collection's keyboard navigation follows.
  - `pub const fn wrapped_grid(columns: usize) -> Self`. Equal-width cells that wrap at `columns`.
  - `pub const fn bounded_grid(columns: usize) -> Self`. Equal-width cells in `columns` columns whose Left and Right stop at the row ends.
- `craft_behavior::NavState`: `pub struct NavState { pub lead: Option<usize>, pub anchor: Option<usize>, pub page: usize, pub wrap: bool, pub column: usize }`. Where a navigation stands between moves.
  - `pub const fn new() -> Self`. A state with no lead, a page of ten and no wrap.
  - `pub const fn at(lead: usize) -> Self`. A state at `lead`.
  - `pub const fn page(mut self, rows: usize) -> Self`. Sets the page size.
  - `pub const fn wrap(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Sets whether moves wrap.
  - `pub const fn column(mut self, column: usize) -> Self`. Sets the lead cell's column.
- `craft_behavior::NavStep`: `pub enum NavStep { Stay, Moved(usize), Expand(usize), Collapse(usize), Column(usize), Unhandled }`. What one move did.

  - `pub const fn new(shape: NavShape) -> Roving`. A group of `shape` with Home and End, no wrap, no paging and no letters.

  - `pub const fn hands_back(mut self) -> Self`. A move the group cannot make reaches the owning component, which may page or change what the group shows.

## `craft_behavior::anchor`

The nine points of a box, shared by the transform box rules and the reference point picker.

- `craft_behavior::anchor::Anchor9`: `pub enum Anchor9 { TopLeft, Top, TopRight, Left, Centre, Right, BottomLeft, Bottom, BottomRight }`. The nine points of a box: its corners, edge midpoints and centre, row by row from the top left.
  - `pub const ALL: [Anchor9; 9]`. Every point, row by row from the top left.
  - `pub fn uv(self) -> (f64, f64)`. Where the point sits in the box: 0 to 1 across from the left edge and down from the top.
  - `pub fn cell(self) -> (usize, usize)`. The point's column and row in the 3 by 3 grid, counted from the top left.

## `craft_behavior::arrangement`

`Arrangement`: a custom placement for a body, in logical coordinates.

- `craft_behavior::arrangement::Arrangement`: `pub trait Arrangement`. A placement of keyed children measured by the caller. Logical: x runs from the start edge of `room`, so
  - `fn place(&self, room: Rect, sizes: &[Vec2], out: &mut Vec<Rect>) -> Vec2`. Fills `out` with one rect per entry of `sizes`, in order, inside `room`; returns the size the placement
  - `fn mirrors(&self) -> bool`. False for a placement that must not mirror (a free-position board).

## `craft_behavior::canvas`

The transform a canvas body is shown through, and the canvas view with its zoom rules, history and ruler units.

- `craft_behavior::canvas::CanvasTransform`: `pub struct CanvasTransform { pub scale: f32, pub offset: Vec2 }`. A canvas body's mapping from canvas coordinates to its layer: `screen = canvas * scale + offset`.
  - `pub const fn new(scale: f32, offset: Vec2) -> Self`. The transform with `scale` layer points per canvas unit and the canvas origin at `offset`.
  - `pub fn to_layer(&self, canvas: Pos2) -> Pos2`. The position in the layer of a canvas point.
- `craft_behavior::canvas::CanvasView`: `pub struct CanvasView { pub rect: ScreenRect, pub center: Point, pub zoom: f64, pub rotation: f64, pub flip_x: bool, pub aspect: f64 }`. How a canvas shows its document: where, how large, turned and mirrored how. Apps convert their
  - `pub fn new(rect: ScreenRect, center: Point, zoom: f64) -> Self`. An upright, unmirrored, square-pixel view of `rect` showing `center` at `zoom`.
  - `pub const fn rotation(mut self, degrees: f64) -> Self`. The same view turned `degrees` clockwise.

## `craft_behavior::codec`

`Codec`: how a text part turns a value into text and text into a value (18.2), with the library codecs of this

- `craft_behavior::codec::Caret`: `pub enum Caret { All, End }`. Where the caret goes when the part takes focus.
- `craft_behavior::codec::Codec`: `pub trait Codec`. A value's text form.
  - `type Value`. The value the text stands for.
  - `const DRAFTS_ARE_VALUES: bool`. Every draft is a value (plain text): a draft applies as typed and never rejects.
  - `fn format(&self, v: &Self::Value) -> String`. The text of `v`.
  - `fn parse(&self, text: &str, start: &Self::Value) -> Result<Self::Value, NotAValue>`. The value of `text`; `start` is the value when editing began, for relative input.
  - `fn parse_tagged(&self, text: &str, start: &Self::Value) -> Result<(Self::Value, Option<u32>), NotAValue>`. The value of `text` and the tag of the unit it named (`Reader::read_tagged`); the default names none.
  - `fn applies_as_typed(&self, _text: &str) -> bool`. Whether `text` applies as soon as it is typed rather than on commit.
  - `fn filter(&self, _c: char) -> bool`. Whether the codec takes `c` as typed.
  - `fn normalise(&self, _text: &str, v: &Self::Value) -> String`. The text the field shows after committing `v`, which was parsed from `text`.
  - `fn caret_on_focus(&self, _text: &str) -> Caret`. Where the caret goes when the part takes focus with this text.
  - `fn sample(&self, current: &Self::Value) -> Cow<'_, str>`. The widest typical text, for the field's ideal width. Codecs with bounded text only; `PlainText` returns
- `craft_behavior::codec::NotAValue`: `pub struct NotAValue`. The text is not a value of the codec's type.
- `craft_behavior::codec::NumberCodec`: `pub struct NumberCodec`. An optional number: empty text is `None`; anything else reads through a [`Reader`].
  - `pub fn new(reader: Reader, decimals: usize) -> Self`. A codec reading with `reader` and writing at most `decimals` decimal places.
  - `pub fn fixed(mut self) -> Self`. The same codec writing every decimal place, trailing zeros included ("1.00"), as a field of a quantity shown
  - `pub fn or_parse(mut self, parse: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<f64> + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self`. The same codec with `parse` as a second reader: it runs when the reader rejects the text, so words of the
  - `pub fn widest(&self, range: &RangeInclusive<f64>) -> String`. The widest text the codec writes for a number in `range`: the longer of its two ends, with every decimal
  - `pub fn decimals(&self) -> usize`. The most decimal places the codec writes.
  - `pub fn reader(&self) -> &Reader`. The reader the codec parses with.
- `craft_behavior::codec::PlainText`: `pub struct PlainText`. Plain text: every draft is a value.

## `craft_behavior::edit_history`

`EditHistory<T>`: the undo and redo of a dialog, a tool session or the keymap editor, fed by edit events.

- `craft_behavior::edit_history::EditHistory`: `pub struct EditHistory<T>`. Undo and redo stacks of a value `T`, at most 100 steps. A gesture is one step: `Begin` opens it, `Commit` closes it,
  - `pub fn new(now: &T) -> Self`. A history that starts at `now`.
  - `pub fn record(&mut self, event: &EditEvent<T>)`. Takes in one event. Events of a gesture other than the open one are ignored.
  - `pub fn is_open(&self) -> bool`. A gesture is open: a caller that restores a draft waits for it to end.
  - `pub fn step(&mut self, now: &mut T, step: Step) -> bool`. Steps `now` back or forward. False when there is nowhere to go. An open gesture is abandoned.
  - `pub fn take_back(&mut self) -> Option<T>`. Takes back the newest undo step without a redo entry, for an edit that was refused: `settled` returns to the
  - `pub fn can_undo(&self) -> bool`. An undo would change something.
  - `pub fn can_redo(&self) -> bool`. A redo would change something.
- `craft_behavior::edit_history::Step`: `pub enum Step { Undo, Redo }`. Which way [`EditHistory::step`] goes.

## `craft_behavior::editor`

`EditorInput`: the one ordered input stream the IME engine produces and an editor part reads (18.4).

- `craft_behavior::editor::EditorInput`: `pub enum EditorInput { Text(Arc<str>), Move(NavMove, MoveMods), Delete { forward: bool, by: Granularity }, DeleteSurrounding { before: usize, after: usize }, Select { anchor: TextPos, focus: TextPos }, Preedit { text: Arc<str>, cursor: Option<Range<usize>> }, ImeCommit, Edit(EditCommand), Newline, Tab, Cancel }`. One input of an editor part.
  - `pub const fn delete(forward: bool, by: Granularity) -> Self`. A `Delete` of `by`.
  - `pub fn preedit(text: impl Into<Arc<str>>, cursor: Option<Range<usize>>) -> Self`. A `Preedit` of `text` with the IME's cursor.
  - `pub const fn select(anchor: TextPos, focus: TextPos) -> Self`. A `Select` of `anchor` to `focus`.
  - `pub const fn is_read_only_safe(&self) -> bool`. Whether the input changes no text: a read-only editor receives only these (18.4).

The fit rules: which tabs, bar items or titles stay in a strip and which go to an overflow menu. [`fit`] and

- `craft_behavior::fit::Fit`: `pub struct Fit { pub shown: Vec<usize>, pub overflow: Vec<usize> }`. Which items the strip shows and which go to the overflow menu, as indexes into the input, in input order.

- `craft_behavior::fit::TabFit`: `pub struct TabFit { pub shown: Vec<(usize, f32)>, pub overflow: Vec<usize>, pub overflow_width: f32 }`. Tabs visible on a strip and tabs that need an overflow menu, all in original order.

- `craft_behavior::fit::fit_items`: `pub fn fit_items(items: &[FitItem], available: f32, gap: f32, section_gap: f32, overflow_extent: f32) -> Fit`. Decides which items stay in a strip `available` long. Everything fits and no item is `Menu`: all items show.
- `craft_behavior::fit::fit_whole`: `pub fn fit_whole(natural: &[f32], selected: usize, available: f32, overflow_width: f32) -> TabFit`. Fit tabs at their natural widths; the tabs that do not fit go to an overflow menu.

## `craft_behavior::flow`

`Insets`, `Flow` and `Axis`: start and end geometry resolved once for the layout direction.

- `craft_behavior::flow::Axis`: `pub enum Axis { X, Y }`. An axis of the screen or of a document. Saved layouts written with the former split axis names load through
- `craft_behavior::flow::Flow`: `pub struct Flow`. The geometry of one rect in one direction. Components place by start and end and never test direction.
  - `pub const fn new(rect: Rect, rtl: bool) -> Self`. The flow of `rect`, right to left when `rtl`.
  - `pub const fn rect(&self) -> Rect`. The rect the flow places in.
  - `pub const fn is_rtl(&self) -> bool`. Whether start is the right edge.
  - `pub fn start_x(&self) -> f32`. The x of the start edge: the left edge, or the right edge under RTL.
  - `pub fn end_x(&self) -> f32`. The x of the end edge.
  - `pub fn span(&self, start: f32, width: f32) -> Rect`. The full-height rect `width` wide that begins `start` from the start edge.
  - `pub fn take_start(&self, width: f32) -> (Rect, Flow)`. Takes `width` from the start edge: the taken rect, and the flow of the rest.
  - `pub fn take_end(&self, width: f32) -> (Rect, Flow)`. Takes `width` from the end edge: the taken rect, and the flow of the rest.
  - `pub fn inset(&self, insets: Insets) -> Rect`. The rect shrunk by `insets`.
  - `pub fn align(&self, size: Vec2, h: Align, v: Align) -> Rect`. A rect of `size` in the flow's rect: `h` along the reading direction (`Min` is the start edge), `v`
  - `pub fn to_physical(&self, logical: Rect) -> Rect`. A rect given in logical x (from the start edge of this flow's rect) as a physical rect.
  - `pub const fn unmirrored(rect: Rect, reason: &'static str) -> Flow`. The same rect placed left to right, for a site that places by explicit rects and must not mirror (a
- `craft_behavior::flow::Insets`: `pub struct Insets { pub start: f32, pub end: f32, pub top: f32, pub bottom: f32 }`. Distances from the four edges of a rect; `start` and `end` follow the reading direction.
  - `pub const fn new(start: f32, end: f32, top: f32, bottom: f32) -> Self`. Insets of `start`, `end`, `top` and `bottom`.
  - `pub const fn horizontal(h: f32) -> Self`. `h` at the start and the end.
  - `pub const fn vertical(v: f32) -> Self`. `v` at the top and the bottom.
  - `pub const fn uniform(all: f32) -> Self`. `all` on every side.

- `craft_behavior::focus::escape_step`: `pub fn escape_step(facts: EscapeFacts, rule: CancelRule) -> EscapeStep`. The order of Escape for the top egui layer that is not exiting: a captured press, the focused component,
- `craft_behavior::focus::key_owner`: `pub fn key_owner(blocked: bool, present: &[OwnerClass]) -> KeyOwner`. Key ownership, in the order blocked, `Menu`, `Recorder`, `Dialog`, `Text`, `Popup` and `PassivePopup`,

  - `pub fn is_finite(&self) -> bool`. Whether the rect and the angle are finite numbers.
  - `pub fn center(&self) -> Point`. The centre the frame turns about.
  - `pub fn corners(&self) -> [Point; 4]`. The corners on the page, turned: top left, top right, bottom right, bottom left of the upright frame.
  - `pub fn part_point(&self, part: BoxPart) -> Option<Point>`. The document point of `part` on the turned frame: a corner or an edge handle, or the centre for
  - `pub fn part_direction(&self, part: BoxPart) -> Option<Vec2>`. The direction `part` points out of the frame, as a document vector turned with the frame; none for the
  - `pub fn quadrant_diagonal(&self, point: Point) -> Vec2`. The diagonal, as a document vector, from the frame's corner nearest `point` towards the frame's centre
  - `pub fn turned_bounds(&self) -> Rect`. The axis-aligned bounds of the turned frame.
  - `pub fn moved(&self, by: Vec2) -> Self`. The frame moved by `by` on the page, its angle kept.
  - `pub fn swapped(&self) -> Self`. Width and height swapped about the centre, the angle kept (X).
  - `pub fn nudged(&self, part: BoxPart, by: Vec2, ratio: Option<f64>) -> Self`. The frame after an arrow key moves `part` by `by`, as a plain drag of the part would, with `ratio` held.
  - `pub fn crop_size(&self) -> Option<(f64, f64)>`. The size Enter would crop to: the frame's own width and height in whole document units, at least 1; `None`
  - `pub fn size_ok(&self) -> bool`. Whether both sides are at least one document unit.
  - `pub fn resized(&self, part: BoxPart, by: Vec2, ratio: Option<f64>, alt: bool) -> Self`. The frame with `part` dragged by the page vector `by`: along the frame's own axes, the opposite edges kept on
  - `pub fn resized_centred(&self, part: BoxPart, by: Vec2, ratio: Option<f64>, alt: bool) -> Option<Self>`. [`resized`](Self::resized) for Auto Center Preview in image mode: `part` dragged by `by` grows or shrinks the
  - `pub fn turned_to(&self, grab: f64, to: Point, step: Option<f64>, image: bool) -> Self`. The frame turned from this one's angle by the pointer's sweep about the centre, from direction `grab`
  - `pub fn within(self, bounds: Rect, resizing: bool, held: bool) -> Option<Self>`. The frame kept inside `bounds`: an unturned frame is cut to it; a turned one is moved by the least that
  - `pub fn level_angle(a: Point, b: Point) -> Option<f64>`. The frame angle (degrees, clockwise on the page, in -45..=45) that makes the line from `a` to `b` level, or
  - `pub fn fitted(&self, canvas: Size, angle: f64) -> Option<CropBox>`. The largest frame with this one's proportions that fits a canvas of `canvas` at `angle` degrees, centred on
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::CropGuide`: `pub enum CropGuide { Thirds, Grid, Diagonal, Triangle, GoldenRatio, GoldenSpiral }`. The composition guide drawn inside a crop frame, in Photoshop's menu order.
  - `pub const ALL: [CropGuide; 6]`. Every guide, in the order `next` cycles through.
  - `pub fn orientations(self) -> u8`. How many distinct orientations `next_orientation` steps through: the Triangle's diagonal runs either way, the
  - `pub fn next(self) -> Self`. The next guide (O); the orientation starts over at 0.

  - `pub fn guides(self, orientation: u8, w: f64, h: f64, grid_step: f64, grid_max: usize) -> Vec<Vec<Point>>`. The guide's polylines for a frame `w` x `h` screen points, in its unit square (0..1 along its top edge, 0..1
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::GuideShow`: `pub enum GuideShow { Auto, Always, Never }`. When the guide shows.
  - `pub const ALL: [GuideShow; 3]`. Every rule, in menu order.
  - `pub fn shows(self, gesture: bool) -> bool`. Whether the guide shows, `gesture` being a crop drag in progress.
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::crop_view`: `pub fn crop_view(b: &CropBox, view: &CanvasView) -> CanvasView`. `view` turned so the frame `b` shows upright (rotation `-b.angle`), the frame's centre kept where it is on screen.
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::crop_view_about`: `pub fn crop_view_about(b: &CropBox, view: &CanvasView, pivot: Point) -> CanvasView`. [`crop_view`] keeping document point `pivot` where it is on screen. `view` itself when anything is not finite.
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::direction`: `pub fn direction(c: Point, p: Point) -> f64`. The direction of `p` from `c`, in radians.
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::drawn`: `pub fn drawn(anchor: Point, cur: Point, ratio: Option<f64>, mods: Mods) -> Rect`. A new frame drawn from `anchor` to `cur`: ⇧ square, ⌥ from the centre, `ratio` held.
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::hit_rect`: `pub fn hit_rect(rect: Rect, point: Point, reach: f64) -> BoxPart`. [`CropBox::part_at`] for a plain upright `rect`, with no frame or view.
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::image_turn`: `pub fn image_turn(frame_angle: f64) -> f64`. The turn the image-mode readout shows: clockwise positive, the frame's angle undone, never -0.
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::normalized_angle`: `pub fn normalized_angle(deg: f64) -> f64`. An angle in degrees brought into (-180, 180]; 0 for anything not finite. An angle already in range comes back
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::screen_step`: `pub fn screen_step(view: &CanvasView, by: Vec2) -> Vec2`. An arrow step `by` (screen points) as the document vector it is in `view`, at one document unit per point.
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::snap_angle`: `pub fn snap_angle(deg: f64, step: f64) -> f64`. `deg` on the nearest multiple of `step` degrees, brought into (-180, 180]; a step that is not finite and positive
- `craft_behavior::geometry::crop::turn`: `pub fn turn(p: Point, c: Point, deg: f64) -> Point`. `p` turned `deg` degrees clockwise on screen (y down) about `c`.

  - `pub fn drag(&mut self, at: DraftPoint) -> Option<DraftEvent>`. The pointer moved to `at` with the button down. Freehand and Mixed: the point is added

  - `pub fn release(&mut self, at: DraftPoint) -> Option<DraftEvent>`. The primary button went up at `at`. Freehand: closes. Mixed: closes unless straight

  - `pub fn close(&mut self) -> Option<DraftEvent>`. Closes an open outline (Enter, a double-click): [`DraftEvent::Closed`] with 3 or more

- `craft_behavior::geometry::draft::turn_to_45`: `pub fn turn_to_45(from: Point, point: Point) -> Point`. `point` turned about `from` to the nearest multiple of 45°, keeping its distance.

  - `pub fn badge(self) -> Option<CursorBadge>`. The badge beside the selection pointer: `Add` a plus, `Subtract` a minus, `Intersect` a
  - `pub const fn name(self) -> &'static str`. The operation's stable name for commands and settings: `"new"`, `"add"`, `"subtract"`,

  - `pub fn filtered(&self, keep: impl Fn(SnapKind) -> bool) -> SnapTargets`. Only the targets `keep` accepts (Snap To with one kind off).

- `craft_behavior::geometry::snap::motion_across`: `pub fn motion_across(along: Axis) -> Vec2`. The document direction a line running `along` an axis moves in: x for a line along `Axis::Y`. A screen caller
- `craft_behavior::geometry::snap::union`: `pub fn union(rects: impl IntoIterator<Item = Rect>) -> Option<Rect>`. The bounding rectangle of `rects`, or `None` for none.

## `craft_behavior::grid`

The `Grid` cursor: columns resolved from last pass's measurements, and the cell each child takes.

- `craft_behavior::grid::GridCursor`: `pub struct GridCursor`. Hands out the cells of one `Grid` pass, row-major. Cells are placed from `last` pass's measurements; this
  - `pub fn new(flow: Flow, columns: &[ColumnWidth], gap: Vec2, last: &GridMeasure) -> Self`. A cursor over `columns` in `flow`'s rect, with `gap` between columns (x) and rows (y), placed by `last`.
  - `pub fn sized_by_content(flow: Flow, columns: &[ColumnWidth], gap: Vec2, rows: &[f32]) -> Self`. A cursor for a measuring run under a body sized from its content: each `Fill` column of `columns` is placed
  - `pub fn cell(&mut self, size: Vec2) -> Rect`. The cell of the next child, whose measured size is `size`.
  - `pub fn room(&self) -> Vec2`. The room the next child measures in: as wide as its column, or for a `Content` column as wide as the columns
  - `pub fn region(&self) -> Rect`. The rect the next child measures and is placed in: from its column's start edge and its row's top, as large
  - `pub fn fill_room(&self) -> Vec2`. The room the next child fills when it fills its width: its column's width, which for a `Content` column is the
  - `pub fn in_content_column(&self) -> bool`. Whether the next child goes in a `Content` column.
  - `pub fn widen(&mut self, size: Vec2)`. The cell last placed measured `size` after all: its column and row record that, and a `Content` column offers
  - `pub fn measured(&self) -> &GridMeasure`. What this pass measured.
- `craft_behavior::grid::GridMeasure`: `pub struct GridMeasure { pub columns: Vec<f32>, pub rows: Vec<f32> }`. What a `Grid` pass measured: the widest child per column and the tallest per row.
  - `pub const fn new(columns: Vec<f32>, rows: Vec<f32>) -> Self`. A measurement of `columns` and `rows`.
- `craft_behavior::grid::resolve_columns`: `pub fn resolve_columns(columns: &[ColumnWidth], content: &[f32], room: f32, gap: f32) -> Vec<f32>`. The width of each column in `room`: `Fixed` is the app's width, `Content` the widest child of last pass

## `craft_behavior::hit`

Hit shapes, their priority and the arena's resolve rule (8.3).

- `craft_behavior::hit::HitLevel`: `pub enum HitLevel { Background, Body, Edge, Handle }`. The tier of a shape inside one stack position; a higher level wins.
- `craft_behavior::hit::HitPriority`: `pub struct HitPriority { pub level: HitLevel, pub sub: u8 }`. A part's rank among the shapes of its stack position: a node editor ranks port above wire above node body.
  - `pub const fn new(level: HitLevel, sub: u8) -> Self`. A priority at `level` and `sub`.
  - `pub const BODY: HitPriority`. The priority of a component body.
- `craft_behavior::hit::HitShape`: `pub enum HitShape { Rect(Rect), Circle { center: Pos2, radius: f32 }, Segment { a: Pos2, b: Pos2 }, Polyline { points: Vec<Pos2>, closed: bool }, Polygon(Vec<Pos2>), OutsidePolygon { within: Rect, polygon: Vec<Pos2> } }`. The geometry a part registers: the shape painted is the shape hit.
  - `pub const fn circle(center: Pos2, radius: f32) -> Self`. A disc.
  - `pub const fn segment(a: Pos2, b: Pos2) -> Self`. A line segment.
  - `pub fn polyline(points: Vec<Pos2>, closed: bool) -> Self`. A chain of segments, closed when `closed`.
  - `pub fn outside_polygon(within: Rect, polygon: Vec<Pos2>) -> Self`. The part of `within` outside `polygon`.
  - `pub fn bounds(&self) -> Rect`. The smallest rectangle around the shape.
  - `pub fn hit(&self, point: Pos2) -> bool`. Whether `point` hits the shape.
- `craft_behavior::hit::LINE_REACH`: `pub const LINE_REACH: f32`. How far from a line a point still hits it, in points.
- `craft_behavior::hit::ResolveFor`: `pub enum ResolveFor { Press, Hover }`. What a resolution is for.
- `craft_behavior::hit::Shape`: `pub struct Shape<O> { pub shape: HitShape, pub sense: Sense, pub owner: O, pub part: Option<PartRef>, pub priority: HitPriority, pub position: u32, pub local: bool, pub min_across: f32 }`. One registered shape of the shape record: a root's, a member's or a part's.
  - `pub const fn new(shape: HitShape, sense: Sense, owner: O, part: Option<PartRef>, priority: HitPriority, position: u32) -> Self`. A shape of `owner` at stack `position`.
  - `pub const fn min_across(mut self, across: f32) -> Self`. The hit area is at least `across` points wide and high.
  - `pub const fn local(mut self, local: bool) -> Self`. The shape lies in the body layer's space.
- `craft_behavior::hit::Winner`: `pub struct Winner<O> { pub index: usize, pub owner: O, pub part: Option<PartRef>, pub sense: Sense, pub local: bool }`. The shape that won a resolution.
- `craft_behavior::hit::resolve`: `pub fn resolve<O: Copy>(shapes: &[Shape<O>], point: Pos2, what: ResolveFor) -> Option<Winner<O>>`. The winner among `shapes` at `point`: stack position first, then level, sub-level, the nearest centre, the last
- `craft_behavior::hit::resolve_mapped`: `pub fn resolve_mapped<O: Copy>(shapes: &[Shape<O>], point: Pos2, what: ResolveFor, to_local: impl Fn(Pos2) -> Pos2) -> Option<Winner<O>>`. [`resolve`] where shapes with `local` set are tested at `to_local(point)`.

What a component takes from the user, which decides its default key use.

## `craft_behavior::intent`

Intents: a key, an AccessKit action or typed text turned into meaning (spec 9.2).

- `craft_behavior::intent::AccessRequest`: `pub struct AccessRequest { pub action: accesskit::Action, pub target: Option<PartRef>, pub data: Option<accesskit::ActionData> }`. An AccessKit action with no intent of its own, or any action on a non-focusable sub-node.
  - `pub const fn new(action: accesskit::Action, target: Option<PartRef>, data: Option<accesskit::ActionData>) -> Self`. A request for `action` on `target`.
- `craft_behavior::intent::EditCommand`: `pub enum EditCommand { Cut, Copy, Paste, SelectAll, Undo, Redo }`. An edit command a component lists in its `KeyUse` to receive it as `Intent::Edit`.
- `craft_behavior::intent::Granularity`: `pub enum Granularity { Unit, Word, Line, Paragraph, Document }`. How far a move reaches.
- `craft_behavior::intent::Intent`: `pub enum Intent { Activate, Submit, SubmitForce, Cancel, Move(NavMove, MoveMods), Delete { forward: bool, by: Granularity, shift: bool }, Space, Digit(u8), Rename, ContextMenu, Reset, Text(Arc<str>), Tab, Edit(EditCommand), SwitchPage { forward: bool }, MoveItem { forward: bool }, Open, Close, Access(AccessRequest) }`. What the runtime routes to a focused component.
  - `pub const fn delete(forward: bool, by: Granularity) -> Intent`. `Delete` of `by`, forward or back.
  - `pub const fn switch_page(forward: bool) -> Intent`. `SwitchPage` to the next page when `forward`.
  - `pub const fn move_item(forward: bool) -> Intent`. `MoveItem` toward the end when `forward`.
  - `pub fn same_kind(&self, other: &Intent) -> bool`. Whether `self` and `other` are the same kind of intent, whatever their data.
- `craft_behavior::intent::MoveMods`: `pub struct MoveMods { pub extend: bool, pub lead: bool, pub by: Granularity, pub fine: bool }`. The modifiers of a move, read from the key table.
  - `pub const PLAIN: MoveMods`. A move by one unit with no modifier.
  - `pub const fn new(extend: bool, lead: bool, by: Granularity) -> MoveMods`. Modifiers with the given parts and no fine flag.
  - `pub const fn fine(mut self, on: bool) -> MoveMods`. The same modifiers with the fine flag set to `on`.
- `craft_behavior::intent::NavMove`: `pub enum NavMove { Back, Forward, Up, Down, PageUp, PageDown, Start, End }`. A logical move. `Back` and `Forward` follow the reading direction; the runtime flips physical arrows
- `craft_behavior::intent::TextPos`: `pub struct TextPos { pub line: usize, pub byte: usize }`. A position in a text document.
  - `pub const fn new(line: usize, byte: usize) -> TextPos`. The position `byte` bytes into `line`.

## `craft_behavior::item_layout`

Item geometry: where each item of a collection sits for a scroll offset and viewport.

- `craft_behavior::item_layout::FreeLayout`: `pub struct FreeLayout { pub rects: Vec<Rect> }`. Items at positions the document stores: a freeform bin or a node graph.
  - `pub fn new(rects: Vec<Rect>) -> Self`. Items at `rects`, in content coordinates.
- `craft_behavior::item_layout::ItemLayout`: `pub trait ItemLayout`. Item geometry in content units. Offsets are `f64`, so a sheet of a million rows places its last row
  - `fn mirrors(&self) -> bool`. False for a layout whose positions are document data (`FreeLayout`); the item step then leaves them as given.
  - `fn content_size(&self) -> ScrollPos`. Finite and non-negative.
  - `fn visible(&self, offset: ScrollPos, view: Vec2, out: &mut Vec<usize>)`. The indices of the items that intersect a viewport of size `view` scrolled to `offset`, ascending.
  - `fn rect(&self, at: usize, offset: ScrollPos) -> Rect`. The rect of item `at`.
  - `fn hit(&self, point: Pos2, offset: ScrollPos) -> Option<usize>`. The item under `point`.
  - `fn nav(&self) -> NavShape`. How arrow keys move through the items.
  - `fn row_len(&self, _row: usize) -> Option<usize>`. `NavShape::RaggedGrid`: the number of items in `row`, rows holding consecutive items and a run of hidden items
  - `fn shown(&self, _at: usize) -> bool`. Whether item `at` takes a place in the layout; a hidden item (in a closed group) cannot take the lead.
  - `fn cells(&self, _at: usize, _offset: ScrollPos, _out: &mut Vec<(ColumnKey, Rect)>)`. The cells of item `at`, with their rects.
  - `fn grid(&self) -> Option<&GridView>`. Column virtualisation and sections of a sheet.
  - `fn frozen_band(&self, _at: usize) -> f32`. The height at the top of the view that item `at` must not paint into: the zoomed frozen-row band for a scrolled
  - `fn first_navigable(&self) -> usize`. The first item that is not frozen: where Ctrl or Cmd with Home goes. Zero for a layout without frozen rows.
  - `fn set_columns(&mut self, _columns: &[(ColumnKey, f32)])`. The column widths the collection resolved, from the start edge; layouts without columns ignore them.
  - `fn column_count(&self) -> usize`. The number of columns of a table or sheet; 0 for a layout with none.
  - `fn column_shown(&self, _index: usize) -> bool`. Whether the column at position `index` is shown; a hidden column has no cell and the arrows skip it.
  - `fn column_key(&self, _index: usize) -> Option<ColumnKey>`. The key of the column at position `index`.
  - `fn column_at(&self, _point: Pos2, _offset: ScrollPos) -> Option<usize>`. The position of the column under `point`.
  - `fn is_auto(&self, _at: usize) -> bool`. Whether item `at` is as tall as it measures itself.
  - `fn measured(&mut self, _heights: &dyn Fn(usize) -> Option<f32>)`. Takes the measured heights of the auto items: `heights(at)` is `None` before the item's first measure.
  - `fn set_height(&mut self, _at: usize, _height: f32)`. An auto item measured `height` in this pass; the items after it move.
  - `fn auto_widths(&self) -> bool`. Whether the items are as wide as they measure themselves. A layout that asks for widths places the items
  - `fn set_width(&mut self, _at: usize, _ideal: f32, _min: f32)`. An auto-width item measured `ideal` and `min` width. The values of the previous pass arrive before
  - `fn set_view(&mut self, _viewport: Vec2, _offset: ScrollPos)`. The viewport and scroll offset the grid view describes. Layouts without a grid ignore it.
- `craft_behavior::item_layout::LineIndex`: `pub struct LineIndex`. Lines (rows or columns) with a default size, the lines whose size differs and their prefix sums, so the start
  - `pub fn uniform(size: f32, count: u32) -> Self`. `count` lines of `size` each (negative or NaN sizes count as `0.0`).
  - `pub fn set(&mut self, line: u32, size: f32)`. Gives `line` the size `size`; the lines after it move. A line at or past `count` is ignored.
  - `pub fn start(&self, line: u32) -> f64`. The start of `line`: the sum of the sizes before it. A line past the last one continues at the default size.
  - `pub fn end(&self, line: u32) -> f64`. The end of `line`: its start plus its size.
  - `pub fn shown(&self, line: u32) -> bool`. Whether `line` exists and has a size above zero.
  - `pub fn count(&self) -> u32`. The number of lines.
  - `pub fn total(&self) -> f64`. The sum of all sizes: where the line after the last would start.
- `craft_behavior::item_layout::SHEET_ZOOM`: `pub const SHEET_ZOOM: RangeInclusive<f32>`. The least and greatest zoom of a sheet (10 %…400 %).
- `craft_behavior::item_layout::SheetLayout`: `pub struct SheetLayout`. Rows of a sheet: one position per row, each split into the sheet's columns, over one [`LineIndex`] for the rows
  - `pub fn new<K>(source: &dyn DisplayOrder<K>, row_h: f32, columns: Vec<(ColumnKey, f32)>) -> Self`. A row of `row_h` per entry of `source`, split into `columns`, each with its key and width.
  - `pub fn lines(rows: LineIndex, columns: LineIndex) -> Self`. A sheet with the size of every row and column in `rows` and `columns`, at 100 % zoom. A column's key is
  - `pub fn frozen(mut self, count: usize) -> Self`. The first `count` columns stay in view while the others scroll.
  - `pub fn frozen_rows(mut self, count: u32) -> Self`. The first `count` rows stay in view while the others scroll. Hidden rows among them take no room.
  - `pub fn zoom(mut self, zoom: f32) -> Self`. Every row height and column width is multiplied by `zoom`, clamped to [`SHEET_ZOOM`].
  - `pub fn rows(&self) -> &LineIndex`. The row extents at 100 %.
  - `pub fn columns(&self) -> &LineIndex`. The column extents at 100 %.
- `craft_behavior::item_layout::SpanLayout`: `pub struct SpanLayout { pub row_h: f32, pub count: usize, pub width: f32, pub columns: Vec<(ColumnKey, f32)>, pub tree: bool }`. Rows of equal height, optionally split into columns: lists, popup lists, tab strips and timeline tracks.
  - `pub fn new<K>(source: &dyn DisplayOrder<K>, width: f32, row_h: f32) -> Self`. One row of `row_h` per entry of `source`, `width` wide, with no columns.
  - `pub fn sized<K>(source: &dyn ItemSource<K>, width: f32, row_h: f32) -> Self`. Rows as tall as `source` says: `Height` and `Rect` are the app's, `Aspect` follows the width, and `Auto`
  - `pub fn columns(mut self, columns: Vec<(ColumnKey, f32)>) -> Self`. The columns with their widths, from the start edge.
  - `pub fn tree(mut self) -> Self`. Rows that expand and collapse.
- `craft_behavior::item_layout::TileLayout`: `pub struct TileLayout { pub tile: Vec2, pub gap: f32, pub count: usize, pub width: f32, pub fixed_columns: Option<usize> }`. Equal tiles that wrap at the width: tile grids, preset grids, font cards and swatch grids.
  - `pub fn new<K>(source: &dyn DisplayOrder<K>, width: f32, tile: Vec2, gap: f32) -> Self`. One tile of `tile` per entry of `source`, `gap` apart, wrapping at `width`.
  - `pub fn in_columns(mut self, columns: usize) -> Self`. Exactly `columns` tiles per row, whatever the width (a user setting; at least one).
  - `pub fn columns(&self) -> usize`. Tiles per row: the user's count, else what the width allows; at least one.

## `craft_behavior::item_marquee`

  - `pub const fn of(mods: Mods) -> MarqueeMode`. The mode a press with `mods` starts: Shift adds, Cmd or Ctrl toggles, Alt subtracts, none replaces.

## `craft_behavior::item_source`

Where a collection's items come from: the data traits and the one way to show a slice.

- `craft_behavior::item_source::DisplayOrder`: `pub trait DisplayOrder<K>`. The order a collection shows its entries in. The library never owns the data.
  - `fn len(&self) -> usize`. The number of entries.
  - `fn key(&self, at: usize) -> K`. The key of entry `at`. Items take their id from it, never from the position.
  - `fn index_of(&self, key: &K) -> Option<usize> where K: PartialEq,`. The position of `key`, `None` when the source no longer holds it. The default scans; a source over a
  - `fn depth(&self, _at: usize) -> usize`. The nesting depth of entry `at`, 0 for a root.
  - `fn has_children(&self, _at: usize) -> bool`. Whether entry `at` has children.
  - `fn open(&self, _at: usize) -> bool`. Whether the children of entry `at` show.
  - `fn is_section(&self, _at: usize) -> bool`. Whether entry `at` is a section header, which navigation skips.
  - `fn section_stop(&self, _at: usize) -> bool`. Whether section `at` is a stop of navigation: it holds the lead, takes Enter, the context-menu key and a
  - `fn section_stops_before(&self, _at: usize) -> usize`. How many section stops (`section_stop`) come before entry `at`. They are not members of the set of items, so
  - `fn enabled(&self, _at: usize) -> bool`. Whether entry `at` can take the lead.
  - `fn text(&self, _at: usize) -> Option<Cow<'_, str>>`. The text type-ahead matches entry `at` against.
  - `fn revision(&self) -> Option<u64>`. A number that changes whenever the entries or their order change; `None` when the source does not report.
  - `fn is_empty(&self) -> bool`. Whether there are no entries.
- `craft_behavior::item_source::ItemSource`: `pub trait ItemSource<K>: DisplayOrder<K>`. A display order that also says how tall its entries want to be.
  - `fn size_hint(&self, _at: usize) -> SizeHint`. The size entry `at` wants.
  - `fn toggled(&self, _at: usize, _column: &ColumnKey) -> bool`. Whether the cell of entry `at` in the toggle column `column` is on.
  - `fn toggle_enabled(&self, _at: usize, _column: &ColumnKey) -> bool`. Whether the cell of entry `at` in the toggle column `column` can be flipped: a locked field's cell shows its
- `craft_behavior::item_source::Keyed`: `pub struct Keyed<'a, T, K>`. A slice as an item source, each entry keyed by `key`.
  - `pub fn with_text(mut self, text: impl for<'t> Fn(&'t T) -> Cow<'t, str> + 'a) -> Self`. The text type-ahead matches each entry against.
  - `pub fn with_toggled(mut self, toggled: impl Fn(&T, &ColumnKey) -> bool + 'a) -> Self`. Whether an entry's cell in a toggle column is on. Without it every toggle cell is off.
- `craft_behavior::item_source::Outline`: `pub struct Outline<'a, K>`. `DisplayOrder` as the key-free outline the navigation core steps over.
  - `pub fn new(order: &'a dyn DisplayOrder<K>) -> Self`. The outline of `order`.
  - `pub fn columns(mut self, columns: usize) -> Self`. The cells of every position, for a sheet's cell navigation.
- `craft_behavior::item_source::SizeHint`: `pub enum SizeHint { Auto, Height(f32), Aspect(f32), Rect(Vec2) }`. How tall (or what shape) an item wants to be.
- `craft_behavior::item_source::keyed`: `pub fn keyed<'a, T, K>(items: &'a [T], key: impl Fn(&T) -> K + 'a) -> Keyed<'a, T, K>`. Shows `items` keyed by `key`: the one way to show a slice (7.1 rule 4).

## `craft_behavior::keys`

The key table (spec 9.2): the one place that maps a key press to an intent and modifiers to `MoveMods`.

- `craft_behavior::keys::DropOperation`: `pub enum DropOperation { Move, Copy, Link }`. What a drop of a dragged payload does with it.
- `craft_behavior::keys::KeyRole`: `pub struct KeyRole { pub resettable: bool, pub space: bool, pub reorderable: bool, pub rename: bool, pub pages: bool, pub nudge: bool, pub type_ahead: bool, pub digits: bool, pub value: bool, pub text: bool, pub moves: bool, pub deletes: bool, pub owner_arrows: bool, pub home_row: usize }`. What the focused stop is, as far as the table's rows differ.
  - `pub const fn new() -> KeyRole`. A role with no special rows.
  - `pub const fn resettable(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Delete resets the control.
  - `pub const fn space(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Space is the component's own intent.
  - `pub const fn reorderable(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Alt+Up and Alt+Down move the lead item.
  - `pub const fn rename(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Rename has its key.
  - `pub const fn pages(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Ctrl+Tab switches pages.
  - `pub const fn nudge(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Arrows are nudges, not moves.
  - `pub const fn type_ahead(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Typed letters are delivered as text.
  - `pub const fn digits(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Typed digits are delivered as digits.
  - `pub const fn value(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Alt with an arrow is a fine move.
  - `pub const fn moves(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. The component acts on the moves routed to it.
  - `pub const fn deletes(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Delete and Backspace are the component's own.
  - `pub const fn owner_arrows(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. The arrow keys are the enclosing component's, not this role's moves.
  - `pub const fn home_row(mut self, row: usize) -> Self`. Ctrl or Cmd with Home goes to item `row`.

- `craft_behavior::scroll::page`: `pub fn page(span: &ScrollSpan, toward_end: bool) -> Range<f64>`. The visible range one visible length further toward the end (or the start), kept inside the content range.

## `craft_behavior::sizing`

  - `pub fn at(size: ControlSize, height: &BySize<f32>, icon: &BySize<f32>, pad_x: &BySize<f32>, gap: &BySize<f32>) -> Self`. The entries of the four tables at `size`.

- `craft_behavior::sizing::Metrics`: `pub struct Metrics { pub padding: Insets, pub min_size: Vec2, pub max_width: Option<f32>, pub gap: f32, pub min_text: f32, pub ideal_width: f32 }`. What the pipeline adds around a component's measured content.
  - `pub const fn new() -> Self`. No padding, no minimum, no wrap width.
  - `pub const fn padding(mut self, padding: Insets) -> Self`. Sets the padding.
  - `pub const fn min_size(mut self, min_size: Vec2) -> Self`. Sets the least size.
  - `pub const fn max_width(mut self, width: f32) -> Self`. Sets the wrap width.
  - `pub const fn gap(mut self, gap: f32) -> Self`. Sets the gap between content parts.
  - `pub const fn min_text(mut self, width: f32) -> Self`. Sets the least text width.
  - `pub const fn ideal_width(mut self, width: f32) -> Self`. Sets the width the component takes where its room allows.

## `craft_behavior::typing`

`TypingRun<C>`: the draft, rejected text, commit, cancel, escape rule and limits of a text part (18.2).

- `craft_behavior::typing::EditRun`: `pub struct EditRun<V> { pub start: V, pub draft: Option<String>, pub error: Option<NotAValue>, pub rejected: Option<(String, V)>, pub composing: bool, pub gesture: GestureId }`. The state a [`TypingRun`] keeps per text part.
  - `pub fn new(start: V) -> Self`. No run is open; `start` is the value shown.
  - `pub fn is_open(&self) -> bool`. A run is open.
- `craft_behavior::typing::Ended`: `pub enum Ended<V> { Commit(V), Rejected, Idle }`. How a commit ended.
- `craft_behavior::typing::EscapeRule`: `pub enum EscapeRule { Restore, Clear }`. What Escape does to a draft.
- `craft_behavior::typing::Escaped`: `pub enum Escaped<V> { Cleared(V), Restore(V), Idle }`. What Escape did.
- `craft_behavior::typing::Typed`: `pub enum Typed<V> { Draft, Apply(V) }`. What typing did to the run.
- `craft_behavior::typing::TypingRun`: `pub struct TypingRun<C: Codec>`. The behaviour of a text part over a codec.
  - `pub fn new(codec: C) -> Self`. A run that applies a draft as typed when the codec says so, with no length limit.
  - `pub fn live(mut self, live: bool) -> Self`. Whether every parsable draft applies as typed, whatever the codec says.
  - `pub fn limit(mut self, limit: usize) -> Self`. The most characters a draft holds.
  - `pub fn escape_rule(mut self, rule: EscapeRule) -> Self`. What Escape does.
  - `pub fn codec(&self) -> &C`. The codec.
  - `pub fn shown(&self, run: &EditRun<C::Value>, value: &C::Value) -> String`. The text to show: the draft, else the value's text.
  - `pub fn open(&self, run: &mut EditRun<C::Value>, start: &C::Value)`. Opens the run on `start` when none is open.
  - `pub fn edit(&self, run: &mut EditRun<C::Value>, start: &C::Value, text: &str) -> Typed<C::Value>`. The draft is now `text`: characters the codec refuses and characters past the limit drop out.
  - `pub fn commit(&self, run: &mut EditRun<C::Value>) -> Ended<C::Value>`. Commits the draft: a value closes the run, a draft that is no value keeps it open.
  - `pub fn commit_tagged(&self, run: &mut EditRun<C::Value>) -> (Ended<C::Value>, Option<u32>)`. [`commit`](Self::commit), with the tag of the unit the committed text named (`Codec::parse_tagged`).
  - `pub fn cancel(&self, run: &mut EditRun<C::Value>) -> Option<C::Value>`. Closes the run without a commit and returns the value from before it; `None` when no run was open.
  - `pub fn escape(&self, run: &mut EditRun<C::Value>) -> Escaped<C::Value>`. Applies the escape rule. A codec that refuses the empty text keeps the cleared draft open under

## `craft_behavior::value`

The value core of chapter 10: the bound every value type meets, `Mixed`, edit events and the list a pass's events

- `craft_behavior::value::Applied`: `pub enum Applied { Changed, Unchanged, Restored }`. What applying an event did to a value.
- `craft_behavior::value::CancelReason`: `pub enum CancelReason { Refused, Dismissed, Escape, Blur, Removed, HostChanged, UndoDuringGesture }`. Why a gesture ended without a commit.
  - `pub const fn document_moved(self) -> bool`. The document changed under the gesture, so the gesture's own edits stay and a revert would also take back the
- `craft_behavior::value::EditEvent`: `pub enum EditEvent<T> { Begin { id: GestureId, before: Mixed<T>, label: Arc<str>, doc_key: Option<DocKey> }, Update { id: GestureId, value: T }, Commit { id: GestureId, before: Mixed<T>, after: T }, Cancel { id: GestureId, reason: CancelReason, before: Option<Mixed<T>> }, Set { before: Mixed<T>, after: T, label: Arc<str>, doc_key: Option<DocKey> } }`. One step of an edit. A pass raises several; [`EditEvents`] holds them in order.
  - `pub fn begin(id: GestureId, before: Mixed<T>, label: Arc<str>, doc_key: Option<DocKey>) -> Self`. A gesture began.
  - `pub fn update(id: GestureId, value: T) -> Self`. A proposed value of the running gesture.
  - `pub fn commit(id: GestureId, before: Mixed<T>, after: T) -> Self`. The gesture committed `after`.
  - `pub fn cancel(id: GestureId, reason: CancelReason, before: Option<Mixed<T>>) -> Self`. The gesture ended without a commit.
  - `pub fn set(before: Mixed<T>, after: T, label: Arc<str>, doc_key: Option<DocKey>) -> Self`. A one-step change, named the way a `Begin` names its gesture.
  - `pub fn id(&self) -> Option<GestureId>`. The gesture the event belongs to; a `Set` belongs to none.
  - `pub fn map<U>(self, f: impl Fn(T) -> U) -> EditEvent<U>`. Maps the carried values and keeps the gesture.
  - `pub fn filter_map<U>(self, f: impl Fn(T) -> Option<U>) -> Option<EditEvent<U>>`. Maps the carried values and keeps the gesture; `None` when `f` has no value for one of them.
  - `pub fn value(&self) -> Option<&T>`. The value the event carries: a proposal, a commit or a set.
  - `pub fn landed(&self) -> Option<&T>`. The value the binding holds after the event: the carried value, or the value a cancel restores.
  - `pub fn phase(&self) -> Option<GesturePhase>`. Where the gesture stands after this event; a `Set` is not a gesture.
  - `pub fn apply(&self, v: &mut T) -> Applied`. Applies the event to `v`: a proposal, commit or set writes its value, a cancel restores the value from before
  - `pub fn apply_mixed(&self, v: &mut Mixed<T>) -> Applied`. Applies the event to a value that may be mixed: the written value is a concrete one.
- `craft_behavior::value::EditEvents`: `pub struct EditEvents<T>`. The edit events of one pass, in order. `push` folds what loses nothing, within one gesture: a later `Update`,
  - `pub fn new() -> Self`. No events.
  - `pub fn push(&mut self, event: EditEvent<T>)`. Adds the next event of the pass, folding it with the one before when that loses nothing.
  - `pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool`. No event happened.
  - `pub fn len(&self) -> usize`. The number of events.
  - `pub fn iter(&self) -> std::slice::Iter<'_, EditEvent<T>>`. The events in order.
  - `pub fn last(&self) -> Option<&EditEvent<T>>`. The latest event.
  - `pub fn value(&self) -> Option<&T>`. The latest value the events carry: a proposal, a commit or a set.
  - `pub fn landed(&self) -> Option<&T>`. The value the binding holds after the events: the latest carried value, or the value a cancel restores.
  - `pub fn began(&self) -> bool`. A gesture began.
  - `pub fn ended(&self) -> Option<&EditEvent<T>>`. The `Commit`, `Cancel` or `Set` that ended an edit in this pass; the latest one.
  - `pub fn cancel_reason(&self) -> Option<CancelReason>`. Why a gesture was cancelled in this pass; the latest cancel.
  - `pub fn map<U>(self, f: impl Fn(T) -> U) -> EditEvents<U>`. Maps the carried values of every event.
  - `pub fn filter_map<U>(self, f: impl Fn(T) -> Option<U>) -> EditEvents<U>`. Maps the carried values of every event, dropping the events `f` has no value for.
- `craft_behavior::value::EditPolicy`: `pub enum EditPolicy { WriteThrough, OnCommit }`. When a write-through source is written: on every proposal of a gesture or only on its commit.
- `craft_behavior::value::Gesture`: `pub struct Gesture<V> { pub id: GestureId, pub before: Option<Mixed<V>>, pub proposal: Option<V>, pub last_given: Option<V>, pub emitted: Option<V>, pub reported: Option<V>, pub dead: bool, pub cancel_seen: u64, pub watched: bool }`. The running gesture of a value component, kept in shown memory under the component. Plain data; the pipeline
  - `pub fn ended(&self, dead: bool) -> Gesture<V>`. The gesture after it ended: no value, the id kept, and `dead` while the press that carried it is held.
  - `pub fn is_running(&self) -> bool`. A gesture is running.
- `craft_behavior::value::GestureId`: `pub struct GestureId(pub u64)`. Names a gesture; ids grow in the order the gestures begin.
  - `pub const fn new(n: u64) -> GestureId`. The id numbered `n`.
  - `pub const fn next(self) -> GestureId`. The id of the gesture after this one.
- `craft_behavior::value::GesturePhase`: `pub enum GesturePhase { Begin, Update, Commit, Cancel }`. Where a gesture stands in one pass.
- `craft_behavior::value::Mixed`: `pub enum Mixed<T> { Same(T), Mixed }`. A value that may differ across a multi-selection.
  - `pub const fn same(&self) -> Option<&T>`. The shared value, `None` while the items differ.
  - `pub const fn or<'a>(&'a self, start: &'a T) -> &'a T`. The shared value, or `start` while the items differ.
  - `pub fn each<const N: usize>(values: [T; N]) -> [Mixed<T>; N]`. Every part of `values` as a shared value: the per-part form of a composite value no part of which differs.
  - `pub fn parts(self) -> [Mixed<T>; N]`. The per-part form of a composite value: a whole `Mixed` binding means every part differs.
- `craft_behavior::value::Validity`: `pub enum Validity<T, M> { Ok, Fix(T), Warn(M), Refuse(M) }`. What a check says about a value. `M` is the message type: craft-ui exports the alias with its own `Message`.
- `craft_behavior::value::Value`: `pub trait Value: Clone + PartialEq + Send + Sync + 'static`. What a bound value must be: cloneable, comparable and shareable across threads.

  - `pub fn value(&self, start: f64, press: &PressState, rtl: bool) -> Option<f64>`. The value the press gives, `None` while it gives none: before a drag starts, and after a cancel. `start` is the
  - `pub fn value_shifted(&self, start: f64, press: &PressState, rtl: bool, shift: Vec2) -> Option<f64>`. [`ValueDrag::value`] for a pointer that warped by `shift` in total ([`DragWarp::shift`]): the pointer

## `craft_behavior::wheel`

Wheel gesture values, as craft-behavior's copy of egui's types so the wheel split is egui-free.

- `craft_behavior::wheel::Wheel`: `pub struct Wheel { pub scroll: Vec2, pub zoom: f32, pub mods: Mods, pub unit: WheelUnit, pub phase: WheelPhase, pub at: Option<Pos2> }`. One wheel event, or the sum of one pass's, as a taker reads it with `take_wheel`.
  - `pub const fn new(scroll: Vec2, mods: Mods, unit: WheelUnit, phase: WheelPhase) -> Self`. A scroll of `scroll` points with no zoom.
  - `pub const fn zoom(mut self, zoom: f32) -> Self`. The same gesture with zoom factor `zoom`.
  - `pub const fn at(mut self, at: Pos2) -> Self`. The same gesture with the pointer at `at`.
- `craft_behavior::wheel::WheelNotch`: `pub enum WheelNotch { Points(f32), ViewShare(f32) }`. The distance one wheel line scrolls a taker.
  - `pub const DEFAULT: WheelNotch`. The distance of a taker that states none.
- `craft_behavior::wheel::WheelPhase`: `pub enum WheelPhase { Start, Move, End, Cancel }`. A wheel gesture's phase.
- `craft_behavior::wheel::WheelRoom`: `pub struct WheelRoom { pub positive: Vec2, pub negative: Vec2 }`. The scroll left in a region, in points of wheel scroll: `positive` is what a wheel with positive `scroll` can
  - `pub const fn new(positive: Vec2, negative: Vec2) -> Self`. A room of `positive` and `negative` points.
- `craft_behavior::wheel::WheelTaker`: `pub struct WheelTaker<O> { pub owner: O, pub rect: Rect, pub horizontal: bool, pub vertical: bool, pub zoom: bool, pub gated: bool, pub scrolls: bool, pub notch: WheelNotch, pub room: Option<WheelRoom> }`. A component that takes wheel input, as registered last pass.
  - `pub const fn new(owner: O, rect: Rect) -> Self`. A taker over `rect` that takes no axis yet.
  - `pub fn for_sense(owner: O, rect: Rect, sense: Sense) -> Option<Self>`. The taker a shape with `sense` registers, `None` when `sense` has no wheel bit: `WHEEL` takes both scroll
  - `pub const fn horizontal(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether it takes horizontal scroll.
  - `pub const fn vertical(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether it takes vertical scroll.
  - `pub const fn zoom(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether it takes zoom.
  - `pub const fn gated(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether every axis needs focus or arming.
  - `pub const fn notch(mut self, notch: WheelNotch) -> Self`. The distance of one wheel line.
  - `pub const fn scrolls(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. The taker is a scroll region (`WheelTaker::scrolls`).
  - `pub const fn room(mut self, room: WheelRoom) -> Self`. The taker's scroll room: it passes a wheel on an axis it has no room left for to the next enclosing taker.
  - `pub fn points(&self, scroll: Vec2, unit: WheelUnit) -> Vec2`. `scroll`, given in `unit`s of the device, as points of this taker's view: a line is one notch, a page is
- `craft_behavior::wheel::WheelUnit`: `pub enum WheelUnit { Point, Line, Page }`. How far one wheel event reaches.
- `craft_behavior::wheel::split`: `pub fn split<O: Copy + PartialEq>(takers: &[WheelTaker<O>], at: Pos2, wheel: Wheel, focused: Option<O>, armed: Option<O>) -> Vec<(O, Wheel)>`. The split rule: each axis of `wheel` goes to the innermost taker under `at` that takes it; a share no taker

## `craft_ui`

The craft UI library: runtime, style, fonts, primitives, the component contract, layout,

- `craft_ui::testing_registered!`: `macro_rules! testing_registered`. Writes `pub fn registered()` for the invoking package: its library crate's registrations in the running binary.
- `craft_ui::InstallError`: re-export of `craft_ui::runtime::install::InstallError`.
- `craft_ui::InstallSpec`: re-export of `craft_ui::runtime::install::InstallSpec`.
- `craft_ui::install`: re-export of `craft_ui::contract::install`.
- `craft_ui::words`: re-export of `craft_ui_macros::words` (outside these crates).

## `craft_ui::contract`

The component traits, context types, `PaintUi`, `Looks`, the pipeline and its step functions, the label, body and item steps, the mount and overlay hosts and `contract::tooltip`.

- `craft_ui::contract::Collection`: `pub trait Collection: Component + HasProps + Meta<Shape = shape::Items>`. A component that shows an item per visible entry of a source.
  - `type Key`. The item key.
  - `type ItemLayout`. Kept in session memory so step 8 can hit-test against last frame's layout.
  - `type Selection`. The selection model.
  - `type Item`. The item component.
  - `type ItemEvent`. Events an item reports.
  - `fn items(&self) -> &CollectionProps`. Plain data: selection mode, reorder, rename, columns.
  - `fn selection(&mut self) -> Option<BindMut<'_, Self::Selection>>`. The selection binding; `None` keeps it in session memory.
  - `fn cell_space<'s>(&'s self, space: CellSpace<'s, Self::Key>) -> CellSpace<'s, Self::Key>`. The grid a model of cells selects in (17.7). The item step passes the grid it knows, the source's rows and the
  - `fn reveal(&self) -> Option<&Self::Key>`. A one-shot request to scroll an item into view.
  - `fn item_layout(&self, source: &dyn ItemSource<Self::Key>, width: f32, cx: &StyleCx<'_>) -> Self::ItemLayout`. The layout of `source` at `width`.
  - `fn item_layout_from(&self, _previous: Option<Self::ItemLayout>, source: &dyn ItemSource<Self::Key>, width: f32, cx: &StyleCx<'_>,) -> Self::ItemLayout`. The layout of `source` at `width`, given the layout of the last pass. An implementor may reuse `previous`
  - `fn ideal_width(&self, _cx: &StyleCx<'_>) -> f32`. The width the collection asks for when it takes its width from its content, over its widest row: a grid of
  - `fn filtered<'s>(&self, _source: &'s dyn ItemSource<Self::Key>, _env: &crate::runtime::env::Env) -> Option<Box<dyn ItemSource<Self::Key> + 's>>`. A view of `source` for this pass, such as the rows a filter keeps; `None` shows `source` as it is.
  - `fn dress<'r>(&self, item: Self::Item<'r>, _row: &RowState) -> Self::Item<'r>`. Adjusts the item the closure built for a row, from the row's state.
  - `fn rename_editor(&mut self, _ui: &mut Ui, _draft: Bind<'_, String>, _label: Rect, _name: &str) -> Option<EditResponse<String>>`. The rename editor at `label`: the label rect the renaming item reports (`ItemOutput::label_rect`), else its row.
  - `fn can_rename(&self, _key: &Self::Key) -> bool`. Whether the item `key` may be renamed; a rename request on another item does nothing.
  - `fn can_drag(&self, _key: &Self::Key) -> bool`. Whether the item `key` may be dragged (reordered, moved in a free layout, or dragged to another collection).
  - `fn accept_from(&self, _from: Id) -> bool`. Whether a drag from the collection `from` may drop here.
  - `fn accepts(&self, _drag: &ListDrag<Self::Key>, _over: Option<&Self::Key>, _insert: usize) -> bool`. Whether the dragged items may land before `insert` (a position of the display order, `over` is the item the
  - `fn takes_files(&self) -> bool`. Whether the collection takes files dragged in from the OS: a drop reports `CollectionEvent::FilesDropped` at the
  - `fn zooms(&self) -> bool`. Whether the collection takes the zoom share of the wheel (Ctrl or Cmd with the wheel): it reports
  - `fn empty_height(&self, width: f32, ui: &Ui, _cx: &StyleCx<'_>) -> f32`. The height of the empty state at `width`: a `Sizing::Content` collection with no rows is this tall. The
  - `fn empty(&mut self, ui: &mut Ui, cx: &mut BodyCx<'_>)`. The empty state at the rect the item step placed. The default shows `CollectionProps::empty`.

  - `const INPUT: Input`. How input reaches the component.
  - `const OVERLAYS: bool`. Whether the component hosts popups: `false` skips the overlay bookkeeping of steps 9, 10, 12, 15 and 16.
  - `const PARTS: bool`. Whether the component declares parts, a text part or an editor part: `false` skips the parts step (the parts
  - `const STOPS: bool`. Whether the component or its parts can take key focus: `false` skips the stop registration of step 7, except
  - `const KEYS: crate::runtime::focus::KeyUse`. Keys the focused component keeps from egui and the shortcut dispatcher.

  - `const SHOWS_LABEL: bool`. Whether the component paints its label as visible text. `false` for a face that shows only an icon or a
  - `const LABELLED_AFTER: bool`. Whether the control draws only its mark and its text is the label step's, placed after it: a check box, a
  - `const SINGLE_VALUE: bool`. Whether the component edits one value that several selected things cannot disagree on (a colour picker's
  - `type Style`. The style type.
  - `type Output`. `Response`, `EditResponse<T>` or a richer type.
  - `fn shows_label(&self) -> bool`. Whether this instance paints its label as visible text; `SHOWS_LABEL` unless an instance prop decides, as
  - `fn paint_outset(_tokens: &crate::style::UiTokens) -> f32`. How far the component paints outside its rect by design, in points (a graph node's ports sit on its edge).
  - `fn style(cx: &StyleCx<'_>, key: Self::StyleKey) -> Self::Style`. Pure function of the resolved context and the key.
  - `fn measure(&mut self, _cx: &MeasureCx<'_, Self>) -> Vec2`. Content size only; the pipeline adds padding and minimum size.
  - `fn fills(&self) -> Option<craft_behavior::Axis>`. The axis along which the component takes all the room the flow offers when that is more than it measured,
  - `fn sense(&self) -> Sense`. The root's sense; the shape's default unless a canvas control.
  - `fn parts(&mut self, _rect: Rect, _cx: &mut PartsCx<'_, Self>)`. Named sub-rects: thumbs, chevrons, handles, segments and the roving groups over them.
  - `fn bind(&mut self) -> Option<BindMut<'_, ValueOf<Self>>>`. Value components return their binding; the same binding on every call, with no side effects.
  - `fn same_value(&self, proposed: &ValueOf<Self>, host: &ValueOf<Self>) -> bool`. Whether `host`, a value the app wrote back, is the value `proposed` as the component holds values: a host that
  - `fn scrub(&self) -> Option<Scrub<ValueOf<Self>>>`. Lets a drag on the field's side label change the value; the pipeline runs the gesture (16.3, B2).
  - `fn scroll(&mut self) -> Option<BindMut<'_, ScrollPos>>`. The scroll offset's owner when the app drives scrolling; `None` keeps it in session memory. The app restores a
  - `fn content_env(&self, _cx: &Cx<'_, Self>, _values: &mut crate::runtime::env::AppValues)`. App values for content the component does not build: its body or its items. Runs once, at step 13.
  - `fn update(&mut self, _cx: &mut Cx<'_, Self>)`. After sensing, before paint: value maths, memory, popup triggers.
  - `fn overlay(&mut self, _ui: &mut OverlayUi<'_>, _cx: &mut Cx<'_, Self>)`. Anchored popups. Runs before flags and paint.
  - `fn flags(&self) -> Flags`. Value-driven flags (checked, open, invalid, busy).
  - `fn activated(&self, cx: &Cx<'_, Self>) -> bool`. When a bound command fires; the default is a primary click or `Intent::Activate`.
  - `fn paint(&mut self, _ui: &mut PaintUi<'_>, _cx: &Cx<'_, Self>)`. Content only; surface, state fill and focus ring are painted by the pipeline.
  - `fn paint_over(&mut self, _ui: &mut PaintUi<'_>, _cx: &Cx<'_, Self>)`. Content above the body or items.
  - `fn access(&self, _access: &mut Access, _cx: &Cx<'_, Self>)`. Value, range, relations, sub-nodes and custom actions beyond role, label and flags.
  - `fn value_text(&self, _cx: &Cx<'_, Self>) -> Option<String>`. The shown value as the component words it ("40.0", "20 to 60"), which assistive technology reads and a viewer
  - `fn finish(self, cx: Cx<'_, Self>) -> Self::Output`. Builds the output from the finished context.

  - `fn placement_changed(&mut self, _change: &overlay::PlacementChange)`. The user moved or resized the container's layer: by the move band, a grip or the keyboard Move and Size

  - `pub fn reorder(mut self, rule: ReorderRule) -> Self`. Items reorder by `rule`.

  - `pub const fn pages(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. The items are pages (`CollectionProps::pages`).
  - `pub fn rename(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Rename runs through the collection's editor.
  - `pub fn rename_column(mut self, column: ColumnKey) -> Self`. A double click on `column` of a renamable item (`rename`, `Collection::can_rename`) opens its rename editor; the
  - `pub fn columns(mut self, columns: Vec<ColumnSpec>) -> Self`. The columns.
  - `pub fn sort(mut self, sort: impl Into<Option<ColumnSort>>) -> Self`. The column the rows are sorted by.
  - `pub const fn activate(mut self, on: ActivateOn) -> Self`. What activates an item with the pointer.
  - `pub const fn striped(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Odd rows carry `RowState.striped`.
  - `pub const fn allow_empty(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. A press on the empty space below the rows empties the selection.
  - `pub const fn lead_on_open(mut self, at: LeadOnOpen) -> Self`. Where the lead starts.
  - `pub const fn lead_follows_hover(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. The lead follows the pointer over the rows.
  - `pub const fn arrows_select(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether the arrow keys select the row they move the lead to.
  - `pub const fn rows_stamp(mut self, stamp: u64) -> Self`. The rows are the set `stamp` names; the lead starts over when the stamp changes.
  - `pub const fn focus_on_press(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. A primary press moves key focus to the collection; off, it leaves focus where it is.
  - `pub const fn space(mut self, action: SpaceAction) -> Self`. What Space does.
  - `pub const fn header(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether the header row shows.
  - `pub const fn digit_keys(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Typed digits go to the collection's `update` as `Intent::Digit`.
  - `pub const fn menu_on_click(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. A plain click on a row asks for its menu, as a secondary click does.
  - `pub const fn item_keys(mut self, keys: ItemKeys) -> Self`. Who takes the arrow keys while the collection has the key focus.
  - `pub const fn marquee(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. A drag from empty space selects with a marquee.
  - `pub const fn deletable(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Delete and Backspace at the lead report the items to delete (`CollectionEvent::Delete`).
  - `pub const fn natural_height(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. The collection takes its full content height and owns no scroll (see [`CollectionProps::natural_height`]).
  - `pub const fn overflow(mut self, axis: Axis) -> Self`. The axis the rows overflow along (see [`CollectionProps::overflow`]).
  - `pub fn current<K: Send + Sync + 'static>(mut self, key: Option<K>) -> Self`. The current item: `Some(key)` marks the item with that key, `None` marks none. `K` is the collection's key type;
  - `pub fn empty(mut self, text: impl Into<Label>) -> Self`. The empty-state text.

- `craft_ui::contract::ColumnAlign`: `pub enum ColumnAlign { Start, End }`. Where a column's content sits.

- `craft_ui::contract::ColumnSpec`: `pub struct ColumnSpec { pub key: ColumnKey, pub header: Label, pub width: ColumnWidth, pub resizable: bool, pub sortable: bool, pub movable: bool, pub frozen: bool, pub kind: ColumnKind, pub align: ColumnAlign, pub tabular: bool, pub first_descending: bool, pub icon: Option<craft_behavior::IconId> }`. One column of a collection.

- `craft_ui::contract::ContainerProps`: `pub struct ContainerProps { pub mount: Mount, pub surface: Option<SurfaceKind>, pub pad: Pad, pub sizing: Sizing, pub room_height: Option<f32>, pub layout: BodyLayout, pub header: Option<HeaderRegion>, pub scroll: Option<Scroll>, pub footer: Option<FooterRegion>, pub end: Option<EndRegion>, pub help: Option<Label>, pub position: Option<SetPosition>, pub focus: FocusPolicy, pub collapse: Option<Collapse>, pub body_gap: Gap, pub least_width: Option<LeastWidth>, pub child_size: Option<ControlSize>, pub arena: bool, pub transform: Option<CanvasTransform>, pub theme: Option<ThemeScope>, pub command_scope: Option<CommandScope>, pub live: Option<Politeness>, pub label: Option<SideLabel>, pub roving: Option<Roving>, pub save: Option<SaveKey>, pub outer_edit: bool, pub collection_body: bool, pub label_column: bool }`. What a container or collection adds to `Common`. A builder added here is added to the forwarder list in
  - `pub const fn new() -> Self`. Props with every field at its default.
  - `pub fn mount(mut self, mount: Mount) -> Self`. Where the container lives.
  - `pub const fn surface(mut self, surface: SurfaceKind) -> Self`. The surface the container paints.
  - `pub const fn pad(mut self, pad: Pad) -> Self`. The padding tier.
  - `pub const fn sizing(mut self, sizing: Sizing) -> Self`. How the size follows the content.
  - `pub const fn room_height(mut self, height: f32) -> Self`. The height the body offers its children in flow ([`room_height`](Self::room_height)).
  - `pub fn layout(mut self, layout: BodyLayout) -> Self`. How the body lays out its children.
  - `pub const fn header(mut self, header: HeaderRegion) -> Self`. A header region.
  - `pub const fn scroll(mut self, scroll: Scroll) -> Self`. A scroll region.
  - `pub const fn collection_body(mut self) -> Self`. The body holds one collection that owns the scroll (a popup list): a layer's own body scroll is off, and the
  - `pub const fn footer(mut self, footer: FooterRegion) -> Self`. A footer region.
  - `pub const fn end(mut self, end: EndRegion) -> Self`. An end region at the line's end edge, outside the body's scroll region. Its stops join the body's roving
  - `pub fn help(mut self, help: impl Into<Label>) -> Self`. The help line the footer shows under the control column. Its text joins the description of the control the
  - `pub const fn position(mut self, position: SetPosition) -> Self`. The container's place in its set (`n of m`), reported on its node.
  - `pub const fn focus(mut self, policy: FocusPolicy) -> Self`. The focus scope.
  - `pub const fn collapse(mut self, collapse: Collapse) -> Self`. Collapse configuration.
  - `pub const fn body_gap(mut self, gap: Gap) -> Self`. The spacing between the children of the body.
  - `pub const fn least_width(mut self, least: LeastWidth) -> Self`. The least width of the content, from a size role.
  - `pub const fn child_size(mut self, size: ControlSize) -> Self`. A scope size for the body.
  - `pub const fn arena(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Makes the container an arena root.
  - `pub const fn transform(mut self, transform: CanvasTransform) -> Self`. Shows the body through `transform` on its own egui layer.
  - `pub fn theme(mut self, theme: ThemeScope) -> Self`. Token-changing values for the body.
  - `pub const fn command_scope(mut self, scope: CommandScope) -> Self`. Makes the container an instance of `scope`.
  - `pub const fn live(mut self, politeness: Politeness) -> Self`. A live region.
  - `pub fn label(mut self, label: SideLabel) -> Self`. A field label.
  - `pub const fn roving(mut self, roving: Roving) -> Self`. Makes the body's members one roving stop.
  - `pub fn save(mut self, key: SaveKey) -> Self`. Saves placement, size and open state under `key`.
  - `pub const fn outer_edit(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. One outer gesture over the body's fields, from open to close.
  - `pub const fn label_column(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. The labelled rows of the body share the widest label as one column. A nested container with its own
- `craft_ui::contract::ContextMenuRequest`: `pub struct ContextMenuRequest<K> { pub key: K, pub at: Pos2, pub column: Option<ColumnKey> }`. A request for the context menu of an item. Collections never open menus: the app does.

  - `pub fn layout_doc(&self, text: &crate::primitives::DocText<'_>) -> TextLayout`. Lays document `text` out `text.wrap` wide: the call `ContentPainterCx::layout` and `MeasureCx::layout_doc`

  - `pub fn fire(&mut self, command: impl Into<CommandRef>, source: FireSource) -> bool`. Runs `command` as this component's own activation would, for a command that is not the component's bound
  - `pub fn part_activated(&self, part: impl Into<PartRef>) -> Option<FireSource>`. Where an activation of `part` came from this pass: `Key` for a keyboard activation while `part` is the
  - `pub fn roving_arrival(&mut self, member: Id)`. Names the member of this container's roving group (`ContainerProps.roving`) that Tab arrives at; the
  - `pub fn focus_part(&mut self, part: impl Into<PartRef>)`. Moves focus to one of this component's focusable parts, in this pass: the rest of the pass reads the new
  - `pub fn focus_own(&mut self)`. Moves focus to the component's own stop (not a part), under the rule of `focus_part`.

  - `type Value`. The component's value type.

  - `fn validity(&self) -> Validity<Self::Value>`. What the binding's check says about the shown value.

  - `fn edit_response(&self) -> EditResponse<Self::Value>`. The `EditResponse` a value component returns from `finish`.

  - `fn set(&mut self, v: Self::Value)`. A one-shot change outside a gesture.

  - `fn cancel(&mut self, reason: CancelReason)`. Cancels the running gesture.

  - `pub fn value(&self) -> Option<&T>`. The latest value the pass's events carry: a proposal, a commit or a set; `None` when nothing was edited or no
  - `pub fn map<U>(self, f: impl Fn(T) -> U) -> EditResponse<U>`. The response of a child component's edit as the response of the edit of a value built from it: the events, the

  - `pub fn separator() -> Self`. A rule between groups of entries. It is not a row: it takes no pick and no lead.

  - `pub fn checked(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether the entry shows as the current choice.

  - `pub fn label(&self) -> &Label`. The label; empty for a separator.

  - `pub fn is_enabled(&self) -> bool`. Whether the entry can be picked; false for a separator.

- `craft_ui::contract::FieldLabel`: `pub struct FieldLabel { pub id: Id, pub rect: Rect, pub press: PressState, pub text: String, pub role: FieldLabelRole, pub detail: Option<String>, pub activated: bool }`. The label a container shows beside its body.
