- `craft_ui::contract::Overlay`: `pub struct Overlay { pub place: Place, pub fit: OverlayFit, pub min_width: MinWidth, pub movable: bool, pub resize: Resize, pub rank: Rank, pub keys: OwnerClass, pub focus: FocusPolicy, pub dismiss: Dismiss, pub motion: MotionSpec, pub opener: Option<Opener>, pub popup: PopupKind, pub size: Option<Vec2>, pub size_limits: SizeLimits, pub placed: Option<Rect>, pub placed_at: Option<Pos2>, pub bounds: Option<Rect>, pub opacity: f32, pub body_scroll: bool, pub moves_window: bool }`. Data describing an egui layer. Presets fill focus and dismissal; builders change single fields.
  - `pub fn popover() -> Overlay`. A popover: a popup anchored below, taking focus in. It closes when the focus moves out of it.
  - `pub fn dropdown() -> Overlay`. A dropdown list: from its content, at least as wide as its anchor and capped, focus stays on the anchor. It
  - `pub fn menu() -> Overlay`. A menu: a popup the arrow keys move through. A press outside closes it and goes no further.
  - `pub fn submenu() -> Overlay`. A submenu: beside its parent entry.
  - `pub fn tooltip() -> Overlay`. A tooltip: below the control, no focus, never takes input.
  - `pub fn dialog() -> Overlay`. A dialog: modal, floating, movable and resizable. `.modeless()` gives rank `Window`.
  - `pub fn modal() -> Overlay`. A modal: centred, content capped.
  - `pub fn modal_chrome(rect: Rect) -> Overlay`. The chrome of a modal over a part of the window the modal covers, placed at `rect`: above the modal, with no
  - `pub fn toast() -> Overlay`. A toast stack: the viewport's bottom end corner.
  - `pub fn floating() -> Overlay`. A floating panel or document window: a window-rank layer that is a pane of the app. F6 reaches it and Escape
  - `pub fn resizable(mut self, on: bool) -> Overlay`. Whether edge grips resize the layer.
  - `pub fn modeless(mut self) -> Overlay`. Rank `Window` and no backdrop.
  - `pub fn place(mut self, place: Place) -> Overlay`. Sets where it sits.
  - `pub fn fit(mut self, fit: OverlayFit) -> Overlay`. Sets how it sizes.
  - `pub fn anchor(mut self, anchor: Anchor) -> Overlay`. Hangs the layer from `anchor` on the side and alignment its preset gave. Without an opener, a point is in the
  - `pub fn size(mut self, size: Vec2) -> Overlay`. Sets the size a resizable layer has before the user resizes it.
  - `pub fn min_size(mut self, min_size: Vec2) -> Overlay`. Sets the least a resizable layer may be resized to.
  - `pub fn size_limits(mut self, limits: SizeLimits) -> Overlay`. Sets the least and the most a resizable layer may be resized to.
  - `pub fn placed(mut self, rect: Rect) -> Overlay`. Places the layer at `rect` in its parent's space. The container reports the moves and resizes the user makes
  - `pub fn placed_at(mut self, pos: Pos2) -> Overlay`. Places the layer's top left at `pos` in its parent's space and sizes it from its content and size limits, so a
  - `pub fn bounds(mut self, bounds: Rect) -> Overlay`. Sets the rect a move or resize keeps the layer inside.
  - `pub fn body_scroll(mut self, on: bool) -> Overlay`. Whether a container mounted as this layer scrolls its body vertically (default on).
  - `pub fn opacity(mut self, opacity: f32) -> Overlay`. Sets the layer's opacity, from 0 (invisible) to 1.
  - `pub fn min_width(mut self, min_width: MinWidth) -> Overlay`. Sets the narrowest width.
  - `pub fn movable(mut self, on: bool) -> Overlay`. Sets whether the user may move it.
  - `pub fn moves_window(mut self, on: bool) -> Overlay`. Sets whether the move band moves the OS window the layer is shown in, in place of the layer.
  - `pub fn focus(mut self, focus: FocusPolicy) -> Overlay`. Sets the focus scope.
  - `pub fn dismiss(mut self, dismiss: Dismiss) -> Overlay`. Sets the dismissal rules.
  - `pub fn motion(mut self, motion: MotionSpec) -> Overlay`. Sets the open and close motion.
  - `pub fn opened_by(mut self, anchor: &Response, trigger: Trigger) -> Overlay`. Records an opener. An anchored layer hangs from the rect of the opener that opened it, on the side and
  - `pub fn opener_anchor(mut self, anchor: Anchor) -> Overlay`. Sets where the layer hangs when the opener named last opened it. A rect or point is in that opener's layer

  - `pub fn id(self) -> Id`. The popup's id: its open state's and its entry in the layer stack.

  - `pub fn bind_open<C: Component>(self, cx: &mut Cx<'_, C>, mut open: BindMut<'_, bool>) -> bool`. Binds the popup's open state to `open`: call it in `update`, after the toggles and closes the component

  - `pub const fn new() -> Self`. An outside press closes; nothing else does.
  - `pub const fn outside(mut self, outside: Outside) -> Self`. Sets what an outside press does.
  - `pub const fn enter(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Closes on an untaken Enter.
  - `pub const fn focus_left(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Closes when focus leaves the scope.
  - `pub const fn on_activate(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Closes when a component inside is activated, except an opener toggling its popup.
  - `pub const fn timeout(mut self, seconds: Option<f32>) -> Self`. Closes after `seconds`.

  - `pub const fn centred() -> Self`. Centred in the viewport.
  - `pub const fn at(pos: Pos2) -> Self`. At `pos`.

A component that opens a free-standing popup, and how. A layer with several openers keeps the first as its
- `craft_ui::contract::overlay::Outside`: `pub enum Outside { Close, Keep, Absorb }`. What a press outside the layer and its anchor does.

Data describing an egui layer. Presets fill focus and dismissal; builders change single fields.
  - `pub fn popover() -> Overlay`. A popover: a popup anchored below, taking focus in. It closes when the focus moves out of it.
  - `pub fn dropdown() -> Overlay`. A dropdown list: from its content, at least as wide as its anchor and capped, focus stays on the anchor. It
  - `pub fn menu() -> Overlay`. A menu: a popup the arrow keys move through. A press outside closes it and goes no further.
  - `pub fn submenu() -> Overlay`. A submenu: beside its parent entry.
  - `pub fn tooltip() -> Overlay`. A tooltip: below the control, no focus, never takes input.
  - `pub fn dialog() -> Overlay`. A dialog: modal, floating, movable and resizable. `.modeless()` gives rank `Window`.
  - `pub fn modal() -> Overlay`. A modal: centred, content capped.
  - `pub fn modal_chrome(rect: Rect) -> Overlay`. The chrome of a modal over a part of the window the modal covers, placed at `rect`: above the modal, with no
  - `pub fn toast() -> Overlay`. A toast stack: the viewport's bottom end corner.
  - `pub fn floating() -> Overlay`. A floating panel or document window: a window-rank layer that is a pane of the app. F6 reaches it and Escape
  - `pub fn resizable(mut self, on: bool) -> Overlay`. Whether edge grips resize the layer.
  - `pub fn modeless(mut self) -> Overlay`. Rank `Window` and no backdrop.

  - `pub fn fit(mut self, fit: OverlayFit) -> Overlay`. Sets how it sizes.
  - `pub fn anchor(mut self, anchor: Anchor) -> Overlay`. Hangs the layer from `anchor` on the side and alignment its preset gave. Without an opener, a point is in the
  - `pub fn size(mut self, size: Vec2) -> Overlay`. Sets the size a resizable layer has before the user resizes it.
  - `pub fn min_size(mut self, min_size: Vec2) -> Overlay`. Sets the least a resizable layer may be resized to.
  - `pub fn size_limits(mut self, limits: SizeLimits) -> Overlay`. Sets the least and the most a resizable layer may be resized to.
  - `pub fn placed(mut self, rect: Rect) -> Overlay`. Places the layer at `rect` in its parent's space. The container reports the moves and resizes the user makes
  - `pub fn placed_at(mut self, pos: Pos2) -> Overlay`. Places the layer's top left at `pos` in its parent's space and sizes it from its content and size limits, so a
  - `pub fn bounds(mut self, bounds: Rect) -> Overlay`. Sets the rect a move or resize keeps the layer inside.
  - `pub fn body_scroll(mut self, on: bool) -> Overlay`. Whether a container mounted as this layer scrolls its body vertically (default on).
  - `pub fn opacity(mut self, opacity: f32) -> Overlay`. Sets the layer's opacity, from 0 (invisible) to 1.
  - `pub fn min_width(mut self, min_width: MinWidth) -> Overlay`. Sets the narrowest width.
  - `pub fn movable(mut self, on: bool) -> Overlay`. Sets whether the user may move it.
  - `pub fn moves_window(mut self, on: bool) -> Overlay`. Sets whether the move band moves the OS window the layer is shown in, in place of the layer.
  - `pub fn focus(mut self, focus: FocusPolicy) -> Overlay`. Sets the focus scope.
  - `pub fn dismiss(mut self, dismiss: Dismiss) -> Overlay`. Sets the dismissal rules.
  - `pub fn motion(mut self, motion: MotionSpec) -> Overlay`. Sets the open and close motion.
  - `pub fn opened_by(mut self, anchor: &Response, trigger: Trigger) -> Overlay`. Records an opener. An anchored layer hangs from the rect of the opener that opened it, on the side and
  - `pub fn opener_anchor(mut self, anchor: Anchor) -> Overlay`. Sets where the layer hangs when the opener named last opened it. A rect or point is in that opener's layer

## `craft_ui::layout`

The layout services: `LabelColumn`, `ColumnGroup`, the `Grid` cursor, `measure_slot` and `arrange`.

- `craft_ui::layout::BodyLayout`: `pub enum BodyLayout { Stack, Row { wrap: bool, align: RowAlign }, Grid { columns: Cow<'static, [ColumnWidth]> } }`. How a body lays out its children, in reading order: mirrored under a right-to-left direction.
  - `pub const fn stack() -> Self`. Children one under another.
  - `pub const fn row() -> Self`. Children side by side from the start edge, with no wrap.
  - `pub const fn grid(columns: &'static [ColumnWidth]) -> Self`. Children row-major over `columns`.
  - `pub fn wrap(self, on: bool) -> Self`. A `Row` that wraps; any other layout is unchanged.
  - `pub fn align(self, align: RowAlign) -> Self`. A `Row` with `align`; any other layout is unchanged.
- `craft_ui::layout::ColumnGroup`: `pub struct ColumnGroup`. The widest content per column key of one scope, measured through the same `LayoutMemory` rule as a `Grid`

  - `pub fn width(&mut self, ui: &Ui, column: &ColumnKey, content: f32) -> f32`. The width every row gives column `column`: the widest content this pass's measuring run found, or in that run

- `craft_ui::layout::measure_slot`: `pub fn measure_slot(ui: &mut Ui, width: f32, body: impl FnOnce(&mut Ui)) -> Vec2`. Runs `body` in an invisible sizing pass `width` wide and returns the size it took. Nothing is allocated in
- `craft_ui::layout::place_label`: `pub fn place_label(ui: &Ui, id: Id, column: &LabelColumn, flow: Flow, label: Vec2, label_baseline: f32) -> LabelPlacement`. Places a label of `label` size with baseline `label_baseline` and the control of the row `id` in `flow`'s

  - `pub fn clear(&self, ctx: &Context)`. Drops every entry (another library opened).

## `craft_ui::primitives::textures`

`TextureCache`, the icon texture path, and beside it the keyed `TextureStore` with its background

- `craft_ui::primitives::textures::TextureCache`: `pub struct TextureCache`. The texture cache of a context: components keep a cache key and ask for the texture when they paint.
  - `pub const CAPACITY: usize`. The number of textures above which the cache drops those no frame asked for lately.
  - `pub fn texture<K: Hash + 'static>(ctx: &Context, key: K, image: impl FnOnce() -> ColorImage) -> TextureId`. The texture for `key`: loads `image()` on the first request and returns the same texture afterwards. A key
  - `pub fn len(ctx: &Context) -> usize`. The number of textures the context holds.
  - `pub fn is_empty(ctx: &Context) -> bool`. Whether the context holds no texture.
  - `pub fn clear(ctx: &Context)`. Drops every texture of the context; the next request loads again.
- `craft_ui::primitives::textures::CachedTexture`: `pub struct CachedTexture { pub id: TextureId, pub size: PixelSize }`. A stored picture as a painter draws it: `painter.image(texture.id, rect, uv, tint)`.
  - `pub fn size_vec2(&self) -> Vec2`. `size` as a vector, for fitting: only the aspect matters to a cell.
  - `pub fn sized(&self) -> SizedTexture`. The texture with its size, the form `PreviewPane` and egui's image widgets take.
- `craft_ui::primitives::textures::FakeThumbSource`: `pub struct FakeThumbSource<K>`. A [`ThumbSource`] for stories and tests in every crate: pictures made from the key and a revision the test sets,
  - `pub fn new() -> Self`. Synchronous square pictures at revision 0.
  - `pub fn later(mut self, later: bool) -> Self`. Renders as `Later` jobs.
  - `pub fn opaque(mut self, opaque: bool) -> Self`. The picture has no transparent quarter.
  - `pub fn aspect(mut self, width: u32, height: u32) -> Self`. The frame's width to height (4:3 landscape, 3:4 portrait).
  - `pub fn failing(mut self, keys: impl IntoIterator<Item = K>) -> Self`. These keys fail.
  - `pub fn blank(mut self, keys: impl IntoIterator<Item = K>) -> Self`. These keys have no picture.
  - `pub fn set_revision(&mut self, revision: u64)`. Every key's revision.
  - `pub fn bump(&mut self)`. Moves every key's revision on by one.

  - `pub fn get_or_put(&self, ctx: &Context, key: K, revision: Revision, make: impl FnOnce() -> ColorImage) -> CachedTexture`. `key`'s picture at `revision`, calling `make` and putting its image only when the entry has another revision

  - `pub fn clear(&self, ctx: &Context)`. Drops every entry (another library opened).

## `craft_ui::runtime`

Per-pass and per-frame services and the one egui adapter.

- `craft_ui::runtime::InstallError`: re-export of `craft_ui::runtime::install::InstallError`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::InstallSpec`: re-export of `craft_ui::runtime::install::InstallSpec`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::install`: re-export of `craft_ui::runtime::install::install`.

## `craft_ui::runtime::access`

`Role`, the access facts of a component and the AccessKit emitter (chapter 12).

- `craft_ui::runtime::access::Access`: `pub struct Access`. The facts a component adds to its AccessKit node: what the pipeline cannot derive from the label, the flags
  - `pub fn role(&mut self, role: Role) -> &mut Self`. The role of this instance, one of the component's `ALT_ROLES`.
  - `pub fn name(&mut self, name: Name) -> &mut Self`. How the node is named, when the label is not the name.
  - `pub fn description(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`. A description, over the bound command's hint and the tooltip.
  - `pub fn placeholder(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`. The hint text of an empty input.
  - `pub fn value(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`. The text value of a text component.
  - `pub fn color(&mut self, color: Srgba) -> &mut Self`. The colour value of a colour well.
  - `pub fn numeric(&mut self, numeric: Numeric) -> &mut Self`. The numeric value of a value component; a step also declares increment and decrement.
  - `pub fn expanded(&mut self, on: bool) -> &mut Self`. Whether a disclosure is open, when no flag says it.
  - `pub fn has_popup(&mut self, kind: PopupKind) -> &mut Self`. What kind of popup the component opens.
  - `pub fn level(&mut self, level: usize) -> &mut Self`. The heading level or tree depth.
  - `pub fn grid(&mut self, grid: GridPlace) -> &mut Self`. The row and column counts of a grid or table node. A collection's item step fills them for its own node.
  - `pub fn position(&mut self, position: SetPosition) -> &mut Self`. The place in a closed set.
  - `pub fn required(&mut self, on: bool) -> &mut Self`. The input must be filled in.
  - `pub fn url(&mut self, url: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`. The target of a link.
  - `pub fn shortcut(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`. The keyboard shortcut, over the bound command's.
  - `pub fn access_key(&mut self, key: char) -> &mut Self`. The mnemonic letter.
  - `pub fn labelled_by(&mut self, id: Id) -> &mut Self`. The node `id` names this one. Targets are `cx.key(..)` ids; one shown in a later frame follows the
  - `pub fn described_by(&mut self, id: Id) -> &mut Self`. The node `id` describes this one.
  - `pub fn controls(&mut self, id: Id) -> &mut Self`. This node controls the node `id` (the popup of an open combo box).
  - `pub fn flow_to(&mut self, id: Id) -> &mut Self`. Reading continues at the node `id`.
  - `pub fn live(&mut self, politeness: Politeness) -> &mut Self`. Makes the node a live region.
  - `pub fn pans(&mut self, vertical: bool, horizontal: bool) -> &mut Self`. The leaf pans its content along the vertical and horizontal axes: the node serves the scroll actions on them,
  - `pub fn busy(&mut self, on: bool) -> &mut Self`. The node is working.
  - `pub fn node(&mut self, part: impl Into<PartRef>, role: Role) -> &mut SubNode`. A node below this component's node, keyed by a `PartRef` (content painters key lines and runs as
  - `pub fn text_selection(&mut self, anchor:(PartRef, usize), focus:(PartRef, usize)) -> &mut Self`. The text selection of the component's own text, from `anchor` to `focus`: each end is a text-run sub-node
  - `pub fn custom_action(&mut self, id: i32, label: Label) -> &mut Self`. An action the component serves itself; it arrives through `cx.intents().take_access()`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::access::GridPlace`: `pub struct GridPlace { pub row_index: Option<usize>, pub row_count: Option<usize>, pub column_index: Option<usize>, pub column_count: Option<usize> }`. The indexes and counts of a grid, table, row or cell node (12.3).
  - `pub const fn grid(rows: usize, columns: usize) -> Self`. A grid of `rows` rows and `columns` columns.
  - `pub const fn cell(row: usize, column: usize) -> Self`. The cell at `row` and `column`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::access::Name`: `pub enum Name { FromLabel, Text(String), Joined(Vec<String>), LabelledBy(Id), Decorative }`. How a node or sub-node is named.
- `craft_ui::runtime::access::Numeric`: `pub struct Numeric { pub value: Option<f64>, pub range: RangeInclusive<f64>, pub step: Option<f64>, pub wraps: bool, pub text: Option<String> }`. A numeric value.

- `craft_ui::runtime::access::PopupKind`: `pub enum PopupKind { Menu, Listbox, Tree, Grid, Dialog }`. The kind of popup a component opens.

  - `pub const fn level(mut self, level: usize) -> Self`. At depth `level`.

  - `pub fn text_run(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>, positions: Vec<f32>, widths: Vec<f32>, rtl: bool) -> &mut Self`. Makes the node a run of text: `text` laid out on one line, each character's start offset from the node's left
  - `pub fn run_link(&mut self, chars: std::ops::Range<usize>, target: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`. Marks characters `chars` of a [text run](Self::text_run) as a link to `target`: the run node gets a `Link`
  - `pub fn level(&mut self, level: usize) -> &mut Self`. The heading level or tree depth.
  - `pub fn value(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`. A plain text value, for a sub-node that shows text as its value and has no number.
  - `pub fn column(&mut self, key: craft_behavior::ColumnKey) -> &mut Self`. The column of a cell of an item row; the item step reads the cell's column index from the collection's
  - `pub fn access_key(&mut self, key: char) -> &mut Self`. The mnemonic letter.
  - `pub fn shortcut(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`. The key that activates the sub-node, as the access tree lists it (`Access::shortcut` for the component's own
  - `pub fn has_popup(&mut self, kind: PopupKind) -> &mut Self`. What kind of popup the sub-node opens.

## `craft_ui::runtime::commands`

The command board: the registry and typed sets, per-frame state, the queue, firing and the gate (spec 11).

  - `pub fn new(allows: impl Fn(&CommandRef) -> bool + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self`. A gate that lets `allows` commands through.

  - `pub fn new() -> Self`. No state, no overrides and no gate.

  - `pub fn overrides(mut self, overrides: Arc<Overrides>) -> Self`. The user's binding overrides; the keymap is rebuilt when the `Arc` changes.
  - `pub fn gate(mut self, gate: CommandGate) -> Self`. The one gate over commands.

  - `fn defs() -> Result<Vec<CommandDef>, CommandDefError>`.
  - `fn no_default_on() -> Vec<(&'static str, Platform)>`. The commands that have no default binding on a platform, as `(id, platform)`.
  - `fn edits() -> Vec<(&'static str, EditCommand)>`. The commands a focused component takes as an `Intent::Edit`, as `(id, edit)`: the `edit` option of the
  - `fn platform_defaults() -> Result<Vec<(&'static str, Platform, Binding)>, CommandDefError>`. The defaults of one platform, as `(id, platform, binding)`: on that platform they replace the command's other

  - `pub fn title(mut self, title: impl Into<Arc<str>>) -> Self`. A label for this frame in place of the command's own, such as "Undo Move".

  - `fn clipboard(&self) -> ClipboardHandle`. The one clipboard path.

  - `fn take_command<S: CommandSet>(&mut self) -> Option<(S, Fired)>`. The next command of set `S` routed to this instance this pass; each is taken once, so a discard pass does
  - `fn resolve_command(&self, command: impl Into<CommandRef>) -> Option<ResolvedCommand>`. `command` as this context shows it: the label, icon and chord with the scoped bindings of the scopes above

## `craft_ui::runtime::cursor`

The cursor service (8.5): requests of a pass collect here, the end hook picks the winner and records it.

- `craft_ui::runtime::cursor::current`: `pub fn current(ctx: &Context) -> Option<Cursor>`. The cursor the last pass of the running viewport settled on; `None` is the default arrow.

## `craft_ui::runtime::drag`

The drag session (8.4): one per context. It holds the payload, the preview, the held modifiers, the operation and the

- `craft_ui::runtime::drag::DragPreview`: `pub enum DragPreview { None, Badge(Label), Frame { size: Vec2, label: Option<Label> } }`. What the ghost layer paints beside the pointer during a drag.
  - `pub fn frame(size: Vec2, label: Option<Label>) -> Self`. A frame of `size` with an optional `label`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::drag::DropMark`: `pub enum DropMark { Line { from: Pos2, to: Pos2 }, Into(Rect), Split(Rect) }`. Where a drop would land, as geometry in the target's egui layer space.
  - `pub const fn line(from: Pos2, to: Pos2) -> Self`. An insertion line from `from` to `to`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::drag::DropTarget`: `pub trait DropTarget<P>`. What a component registers with `Pointer::drop_target`.
  - `type Drop`. What a drop on this target carries to the component's caller.
  - `fn accept(&self, payload: &P, at: Pos2, op: DropOperation) -> Option<Self::Drop>`. `None` rejects. `at` is the pointer in the component's egui layer space, so a target picks its sub-area (a port,
  - `fn mark(&self, drop: &Self::Drop) -> DropMark`. Where `drop` would land, a value the ghost layer paints.
- `craft_ui::runtime::drag::OsFile`: `pub struct OsFile { pub name: String, pub path: Option<PathBuf>, pub mime: String, pub bytes: Option<Arc<[u8]>> }`. A file the OS drags over the window or drops on it.
- `craft_ui::runtime::drag::OsFiles`: `pub struct OsFiles { pub files: Vec<OsFile> }`. The payload of a drag that comes from the OS: files held over the window, then the files dropped. Targets take it
- `craft_ui::runtime::drag::WindowDropTarget`: `pub trait WindowDropTarget<P>`. What a component registers with `Pointer::window_drop_target`: a target over the whole window, which needs no
  - `type Drop`. What a drop on this target carries to the component's caller.
  - `fn accept(&self, payload: &P, op: DropOperation) -> Option<Self::Drop>`. `None` rejects. `op` is the operation the held modifiers pick, which a target may refuse.
  - `fn mark(&self, drop: &Self::Drop) -> DropMark`. Where `drop` would land, a value the ghost layer paints.
- `craft_ui::runtime::drag::active`: `pub fn active<P: Send + Sync + 'static>(ctx: &Context) -> Option<Arc<P>>`. The active drag payload of type `P`, from any viewport. For code without a `Cx`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::drag::cancel`: `pub fn cancel(ctx: &Context, reason: CancelReason)`. Ends the session without a drop; the source's press ends with it, so nobody gets a drop.

## `craft_ui::runtime::egui::access`

AccessKit node building, `register_accesskit_parent` and `set_accesskit_focus`: the one place that names

- `craft_ui::runtime::egui::access::AccessTree`: `pub struct AccessTree`. The AccessKit tree update of one frame.
  - `pub fn has(&self, id: Id) -> bool`. Whether the node of `id` exists.
  - `pub fn role(&self, id: Id) -> Option<Role>`. The role of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn name(&self, id: Id) -> Option<&str>`. The name of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn description(&self, id: Id) -> Option<&str>`. The description of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn description_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The description of a node by key.
  - `pub fn shortcut_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The keyboard shortcut a node by key lists.
  - `pub fn value_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The text value of a node by key.
  - `pub fn value(&self, id: Id) -> Option<&str>`. The text value of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn numeric(&self, id: Id) -> Option<Numeric>`. The numeric value and range of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn is_disabled(&self, id: Id) -> bool`. Whether the node of `id` is reported as disabled.
  - `pub fn declares(&self, id: Id, action: Declared) -> bool`. Whether the node of `id` declares `action`.
  - `pub fn labelled_by(&self, id: Id) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. The nodes that name the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn labelled_by_key(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. [`AccessTree::labelled_by`] for a node by key.
  - `pub fn described_by(&self, id: Id) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. The nodes that describe the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn position(&self, id: Id) -> Option<(usize, usize)>`. The place of the node of `id` in its set: its zero-based index and the size of the set.
  - `pub fn grid_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> GridPlace`. The row and column indexes and counts of a node by key.
  - `pub fn sort_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<bool>`. The sort order a column header node by key shows: `true` descending.
  - `pub fn declares_sort(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether a column header node declares a sort direction: it sorts when activated, sorted now or not.
  - `pub fn expanded(&self, id: Id) -> Option<bool>`. The expanded state of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn has_popup(&self, id: Id) -> Option<PopupKind>`. The kind of popup the node of `id` opens.
  - `pub fn controls(&self, id: Id) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. The nodes the node of `id` controls.
  - `pub fn is_modal(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether the node of `key` is modal.
  - `pub fn live(&self, id: Id) -> Option<Politeness>`. The live politeness of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn invalid(&self, id: Id) -> bool`. Whether the node of `id` is marked invalid.
  - `pub fn run_texts(&self, id: Id) -> Vec<String>`. The text of each `TextRun` node below `id`, in order.
  - `pub fn text_selection(&self, id: Id) -> Option<((NodeKey, usize),(NodeKey, usize))>`. The selection of the text node `id` as the run node and character index of its anchor and focus.
  - `pub fn name_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The name of a node by key.
  - `pub fn toggled_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<craft_behavior::Mixed<bool>>`. The toggled state of a node by key.
  - `pub fn expanded_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<bool>`. The expanded state of a node by key.
  - `pub fn has_popup_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<PopupKind>`. The kind of popup the node of `key` opens.
  - `pub fn access_key_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<char>`. The access key of the node of `key`.
  - `pub fn current_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether the node of `key` is the current item of its set (the current page).
  - `pub fn parent_of(&self, id: Id) -> Option<NodeKey>`. The nearest ancestor of `id` that screen readers see: AccessKit's consumer drops generic containers.
  - `pub fn parent_of_key(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<NodeKey>`. [`AccessTree::parent_of`] for a node by key.
  - `pub fn in_ltr_axis(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether the node is, or lies inside, a component whose horizontal axis keeps its left-to-right order
  - `pub fn keys(&self) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. Every node that assistive technology reports.
  - `pub fn node_count(&self) -> usize`. The number of nodes that assistive technology reports.
  - `pub fn role_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<Role>`. The role of a node by key.
  - `pub fn selected_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<bool>`. Whether a node by key is reported as selected: `None` for a node that reports no selected state.
  - `pub fn bounds_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<egui::Rect>`. The bounds of a node by key, in points.
  - `pub fn is_disabled_key(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether a node by key is reported as disabled.
  - `pub fn declares_key(&self, key: NodeKey, action: Declared) -> bool`. Whether a node by key declares `action`.
  - `pub fn children_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. The children of a node by key.
- `craft_ui::runtime::egui::access::Declared`: `pub enum Declared { Focus, Click, Increment, Decrement, SetValue, SetTextSelection, Expand, Collapse, Custom, ScrollUp, ScrollDown, ScrollLeft, ScrollRight }`. An action a node declares.
- `craft_ui::runtime::egui::access::NodeKey`: `pub struct NodeKey`. A node's number in the tree.
  - `pub fn of(id: Id) -> NodeKey`. The key of the node of `id`.

- `craft_ui::runtime::egui::areas::layer_order`: `pub fn layer_order() -> LayerOrder`. Two frames over three areas; the second lists the first area and an unknown layer.

## `craft_ui::runtime::egui::screenshot`

One-shot screenshot slots and the facts a screenshot is measured against.

- `craft_ui::runtime::egui::screenshot::ScreenshotError`: `pub enum ScreenshotError { DimensionsOverflow { width: usize, height: usize }, MalformedImage { width: usize, height: usize, pixels: usize }, ImageTooLarge { bytes: usize, max_bytes: usize } }`. Why a completed screenshot was rejected and released.
- `craft_ui::runtime::egui::screenshot::ScreenshotFacts`: `pub struct ScreenshotFacts { pub size_points: Vec2, pub pixels_per_point: f32, pub zoom: f32, pub max_texture_side: usize, pub minimized: bool }`. What a screenshot of the running viewport measures this pass.
- `craft_ui::runtime::egui::screenshot::ScreenshotSlot`: `pub struct ScreenshotSlot`. The live request side of a screenshot. Keep it alive until the result is taken or the request is cancelled;
  - `pub fn take(&self) -> Option<Result<Arc<ColorImage>, ScreenshotError>>`. Takes the completed result, at most once.
  - `pub fn cancel(&self)`. Cancels the request and releases any completed image. A completion that published its result before this
  - `pub fn detached(ctx: &Context, viewport: ViewportId, max_image_bytes: usize) -> (Self, ScreenshotCallback)`. A slot with its callback and no command sent, for trials that stand in for the renderer.
  - `pub fn complete(&self, image: Arc<ColorImage>)`. Completes the slot with `image` as the renderer would, without the callback.
  - `pub fn lock_is_free(&self) -> bool`. Whether the slot mutex is free, proving the wakeup runs after the lock was released.

## `craft_ui::runtime::env`

`Env` and scopes, app values and `content_env`.

- `craft_ui::runtime::env::Env`: `pub struct Env`. The inherited context of a `Ui`: one `Arc` clone per read.
  - `pub fn words<L: WordSet + Send + Sync + 'static>(&self) -> Arc<L>`. The word set `L` in the scope's language.
  - `pub fn format(&self) -> &Format`. How the scope writes numbers: locale digits, separators and grouping.
  - `pub fn dates(&self) -> Arc<DateFormat>`. How the scope writes dates: the host's catalog format, else the system region's.
  - `pub fn today(&self) -> Option<CalendarDate>`. Today's date as the host supplied it with `RootEnv::today`; `None` when it gave none.
  - `pub fn units(&self) -> Units`. The scope's number format and unit words, to read and write numbers with their unit.
  - `pub fn relative_age(&self, seconds: u64, style: AgeStyle) -> String`. How long ago `seconds` was, in the scope's language and digits: "Just now", "3 minutes ago", "Yesterday".
  - `pub fn of(ui: &Ui) -> Env`. The env of `ui`: the nearest entry up the `Ui` stack, else the running viewport's root env, so the `Ui`
  - `pub fn get<T: EnvValue>(&self) -> T`. A clone of the innermost app value, else `T::root()`. Large values hold an `Arc`.
  - `pub fn capture(&self) -> EnvCapture`. The inherited group only; layer-local fields start fresh where the capture is installed.
  - `pub fn view(&self) -> EnvView<'_>`. What component code sees of this env.
  - `pub fn folder(&self) -> Folder`. Folds text for search in the scope's language: case and accents ignored, dotted and dotless i by locale.
  - `pub fn collator(&self) -> Arc<Collator>`. Orders text in the scope's language, with runs of digits by value (`Layer 2` before `Layer 10`).
- `craft_ui::runtime::env::EnvCapture`: `pub struct EnvCapture`. The inherited group of an env, captured to open a popup or viewport: the layer-local fields are reset.
- `craft_ui::runtime::env::Scoped`: `pub struct Scoped { pub ink: Ink, pub size: ControlSize, pub enabled: bool, pub read_only: bool, pub wheel_gated: bool, pub direction: Direction, pub text_direction: Direction, pub motion: MotionPolicy, pub ids_stable: bool, pub scale: Scale, pub arena: Option<ArenaId>, pub transform: Option<CanvasTransform> }`. The values of an env.

  - `pub fn set<T: EnvValue>(&mut self, value: T) -> &mut Self`. Sets the value of type `T`.

- `craft_ui::runtime::env::Counted`: re-export of `craft_i18n::Counted` (outside these crates).
- `craft_ui::runtime::env::Direction`: re-export of `craft_i18n::Direction` (outside these crates).
- `craft_ui::runtime::env::EnvValue`: re-export of `craft_ui_macros::EnvValue` (outside these crates).

  - `pub fn words<L: WordSet + Send + Sync + 'static>(&self) -> Arc<L>`. The word set `L` in the scope's language, built once per `Words`.
  - `pub fn format(&self) -> &'a Format`. How the scope writes numbers: locale digits, separators and grouping.
  - `pub fn dates(&self) -> Arc<DateFormat>`. How the scope writes dates: the host's catalog format, else the system region's.
  - `pub fn today(&self) -> Option<CalendarDate>`. Today's date as the host supplied it with `RootEnv::today`; `None` when it gave none.
  - `pub fn units(&self) -> Units`. The scope's number format and unit words, to read and write numbers with their unit.
  - `pub fn relative_age(&self, seconds: u64, style: AgeStyle) -> String`. How long ago `seconds` was, in the scope's language and digits: "Just now", "3 minutes ago", "Yesterday".

  - `pub const fn points_per_inch(self) -> f64`. How many points make an inch.

  - `pub const NONE: FocusPolicy`. A scope that changes nothing.
  - `pub const PANE: FocusPolicy`. A pane: an F6 stop.
  - `pub const DIALOG: FocusPolicy`. A dialog: Tab stays inside, Enter submits, Escape closes.
  - `pub const POPUP: FocusPolicy`. A popup: focus moves in, Escape closes and focus returns to the opener.

  - `pub const fn restore(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether focus returns to the opener.
  - `pub const fn initial(mut self, initial: Initial) -> Self`. Where focus goes first.
  - `pub const fn cancel(mut self, cancel: CancelRule) -> Self`. What Escape does.
  - `pub const fn pages(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether Ctrl+Tab cycles pages.

- `craft_ui::runtime::focus::Initial`: `pub enum Initial { Keep, First, Default, Key(&'static str), KeepAnchor }`. Where focus goes the first time a scope is pushed.

  - `pub fn take_digit(&mut self) -> Option<u8>`. Takes the next typed digit.

  - `pub fn take_exact(&mut self, intent: &Intent) -> bool`. Takes an intent equal to `intent` in every field, once per pass: `Intent::delete(false, Granularity::Grapheme)`

  - `pub fn take_scroll_access(&mut self, into_view: bool) -> Option<AccessRequest>`. Takes the next AccessKit request that asks a scroll region to scroll, or with `into_view` to show its target.
  - `pub fn take_edit(&mut self) -> Option<EditCommand>`. Takes the next edit command.
  - `pub fn take_text(&mut self) -> Option<Arc<str>>`. Takes the next typed text.
  - `pub fn take_nudge(&mut self) -> Option<(Vec2, Mods)>`. Takes the physical arrow nudge for canvas content: a unit direction and the modifiers.

  - `pub fn take_access(&mut self) -> Option<AccessRequest>`. Takes the next `Intent::Access` request.
- `craft_ui::runtime::focus::KeepKeys`: `pub enum KeepKeys { None, Tab, InlineArrows, BlockArrows }`. The keys a focused component keeps from the key stage.

A component's declared keys.
  - `pub const TEXT_MULTILINE: KeyUse`. A multi-line text field: Up and Down move the caret.

  - `pub const SPLITTER: KeyUse`. A divider between panes: it takes the arrows as moves of its value, but it is no document and no canvas tool, so
  - `pub const RECORDER: KeyUse`. A shortcut recorder: it takes the arrows of a canvas, but it is no document, so it marks no window and a Tab in
  - `pub const fn of(input: Input) -> KeyUse`. The default key use of a component that takes `input`. The only place egui key filters start from.

  - `pub const fn with_value(mut self, value: bool) -> Self`. The same use with `value` as whether its arrows step a value.
  - `pub const fn with_moves(mut self) -> Self`. The same use declaring that the component acts on the moves routed to it.
  - `pub const fn with_deletes(mut self) -> Self`. The same use declaring that the component acts on Delete and Backspace.
  - `pub const fn with_submits(mut self) -> Self`. The same use declaring that the component acts on Enter and takes `Intent::Submit`.

  - `pub fn claims_chord(&self, chord: &Chord) -> bool`. Whether the component claims `chord`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::focus::Letters`: `pub enum Letters { None, TypeAhead }`. Whether a roving group matches typed letters to entries.
- `craft_ui::runtime::focus::Roving`: `pub struct Roving { pub shape: NavShape, pub wrap: bool, pub home_end: bool, pub page: Option<usize>, pub letters: Letters, pub arrows: Arrows, pub first_column: Option<usize>, pub hands_back: bool, pub no_lead: bool }`. A roving group's rules: one Tab stop whose members take moves.
  - `pub const fn new(shape: NavShape) -> Roving`. A group of `shape` with Home and End, no wrap, no paging and no letters.

  - `pub const fn hands_back(mut self) -> Self`. A move the group cannot make reaches the owning component, which may page or change what the group shows.
  - `pub const fn no_lead(mut self, on: bool) -> Self`. Whether the focused member is no lead: the next move starts at an end, as from a group with no lead.

## `craft_ui::runtime::memory`

Ids and memory: `Remember`, the memory stage, `Facts`, eviction and saved state.

- `craft_ui::runtime::memory::Remember`: `pub trait Remember`. Implemented by `Cx`, `BodyCx` and `ItemCx` over the pipeline's memory stage, each keyed by its own base id.
  - `fn key(&self, part: impl Hash + Debug) -> Id`. `base.with(part)`: child and relation ids.
  - `fn memory<T: Slot>(&mut self) -> &mut T`. The shown slot of this component.
  - `fn memory_at<T: Slot>(&mut self, part: impl Hash + Debug) -> &mut T`. A shown slot keyed `sub(base, ("mem", part))`.
  - `fn take<T: Slot>(&mut self) -> Option<T>`. The shown slot, read once: it moves out, so it follows the shown lifetime's eviction.
  - `fn press_slot<T: Slot>(&mut self) -> &mut Option<T>`. The press slot: it goes when its press ends.
  - `fn keep<T: Slot>(&mut self) -> &mut T`. The session slot: it goes only through `forget`.
  - `fn saved<T: Savable>(&mut self, key: &SaveKey) -> &mut T`. The saved slot of `key`: it goes only through `forget_saved`.
  - `fn recall<T: Slot>(&self) -> T`. A read-only copy of the shown slot, for paint.
  - `fn pending<K: Hash, T: Slot, E: Slot>(&mut self, key: K, start: impl FnOnce() -> Job<T, E>) -> &Pending<T, E>`. The result of the job `start` returns, one per `key`: it is submitted once, cancelled when the owner is
  - `fn appeared(&self) -> bool`. True on the first pass a shown stage exists for this id.
- `craft_ui::runtime::memory::Savable`: `pub trait Savable: Slot + Serialize + DeserializeOwned`. A slot that also lives in saved state.
- `craft_ui::runtime::memory::SaveKey`: `pub struct SaveKey`. The key of a saved slot, such as `"craft_dialogs.export.place"`. The crate prefix is checked, and the key
  - `pub const fn new(key: &'static str) -> Self`. A key `crate_prefix.name`; evaluated in a const context it rejects a malformed name at compile time. The
  - `pub fn named(key: impl Into<Arc<str>>) -> Self`. A key `crate_prefix.name` built at run time, for slots kept per item, such as a window for each open
  - `pub fn as_str(&self) -> &str`. The key's text.
- `craft_ui::runtime::memory::Slot`: `pub trait Slot: Clone + Default + Send + Sync + 'static`. A value kept in memory. `Clone` is required because egui's temp store clones on insert; large caches hold `Arc`s.

## `craft_ui::runtime::motion`

The motion service (13): tweens over one clock, the motion table, and one repaint request per viewport.

- `craft_ui::runtime::motion::Animate`: `pub trait Animate: crate::runtime::memory::sealed::Sealed`. Motion on `Cx`, `BodyCx` and `ItemCx`. Every method takes `&self`: slots live in context data under the
  - `fn anim<T: Interpolate>(&self, name: &'static str, target: T, spec: MotionSpec) -> T`. `target` approached over `spec`'s duration; the first call returns `target` without animating.
  - `fn anim_bool(&self, name: &'static str, on: bool, spec: MotionSpec) -> f32`. A 0 to 1 value approaching 1 when `on`, else 0.
  - `fn presence(&self, name: &'static str, open: bool, spec: MotionSpec) -> Presence`. Open-close presence for something shown while it opens and fades out.
  - `fn jump<T: Interpolate>(&self, name: &'static str, value: T)`. Sets `name` to `value` with no animation.
  - `fn phase(&self, period: f64, tick: Option<f64>) -> Option<f32>`. The 0 to 1 position of a loop of `period` seconds, stepped every `tick` seconds when given; `None` when
  - `fn blink(&self, restart: f64) -> bool`. Whether a caret last moved at `restart` (a pass time) shows now: solid right after `restart`, then
  - `fn now(&self) -> f64`. The pass's time in seconds, read once per frame.
  - `fn wake_at(&self, at: f64)`. Asks for a pass at `at` (timers, holds, polls), whatever the motion mode.
- `craft_ui::runtime::motion::Interpolate`: `pub trait Interpolate: Copy + PartialEq + Send + Sync + 'static`. A value a tween can run over.
  - `fn lerp(a: Self, b: Self, t: f32) -> Self`. The value `t` of the way from `a` to `b`; `t` is 0 to 1.
  - `fn distance(a: Self, b: Self) -> f32`. How far apart two values are, in the value's own unit.
- `craft_ui::runtime::motion::MotionSpec`: `pub struct MotionSpec`. A motion constant: a component picks one from `spec`, never writes seconds.
  - `pub const fn kind(self) -> MotionKind`. The kind that picks this spec's row of the motion table.
- `craft_ui::runtime::motion::Presence`: `pub struct Presence { pub t: f32, pub visible: bool, pub exiting: bool }`. An open-close presence: `t` is 0 closed and 1 open, and a closing one stays visible until `t` reaches 0.
- `craft_ui::runtime::motion::Tween`: `pub struct Tween<T: Interpolate>`. A running animation of one value. Evaluating it at the same time twice gives the same value, so a
  - `pub fn at(value: T) -> Self`. A tween at rest at `value`.
  - `pub fn shown(&self) -> T`. The value to paint.
  - `pub fn target(&self) -> T`. The value it runs toward.
  - `pub fn is_settled(&self) -> bool`. True once the shown value is the target.
  - `pub fn retarget(&mut self, to: T, now: f64)`. Runs toward `to` from the value shown now.
- `craft_ui::runtime::motion::now`: `pub fn now() -> web_time::Instant`. The wall clock's reading, for a host that drives a store on real time (`ShellApp::tick`). It is the one place

- `craft_ui::runtime::overlay::family`: `pub fn family(ctx: &Context, id: Id) -> SmallVec<[Id; 4]>`. `id` and everything opened from inside it, shown last frame.
- `craft_ui::runtime::overlay::is_open`: `pub fn is_open(ctx: &Context, id: Id) -> bool`. Whether the layer `id` was shown last frame.

- `craft_ui::runtime::overlay::top`: `pub fn top(ctx: &Context) -> Option<Id>`. The layer that receives Cancel: the newest member of the topmost family that takes keys.

## `craft_ui::runtime::pending`

Background results (14): one result per key in runtime memory, produced by a job on a `host::queue::JobQueue` per context.

- `craft_ui::runtime::pending::Job`: `pub struct Job<T, E>`. Background work for `Remember::pending`: runs on the context's job queue, stops early when its owner is
  - `pub fn new(run: impl FnOnce(&Progress, &Cancel) -> Result<T, E> + Send + 'static) -> Self`. A job at `Priority::VISIBLE`. `run` checks `Cancel::is_cancelled` where it can stop early.
  - `pub fn priority(mut self, priority: Priority) -> Self`. Runs before waiting jobs of lower priority.
- `craft_ui::runtime::pending::LOST_LIMIT`: `pub const LOST_LIMIT: u32`. How many times in a row a job may end without delivering before its key shows [`Pending::Lost`].
- `craft_ui::runtime::pending::Pending`: `pub enum Pending<T, E> { Loading { progress: Option<f32> }, Ready(T), Failed(E), Lost }`. A result that may not have arrived.
- `craft_ui::runtime::pending::Progress`: `pub struct Progress`. A job's report of how far it has come. A report asks for a pass, so the progress shows.
  - `pub fn report(&self, fraction: f32)`. Reports `fraction` (0 to 1) done.

  - `pub fn new(key: impl Into<String>) -> Self`. A request for the dropper of `key`.

  - `pub fn new(key: impl Into<String>, kind: PathKind) -> Self`. A request for the path of `key`.

## `craft_ui::runtime::toast`

The toast queue: one per viewport, filled from any code with a `Context`.

- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::MAX_CARDS`: `pub const MAX_CARDS: usize`. Most cards queued at once; posting past it drops the oldest card that has no action.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::TIMEOUT`: `pub const TIMEOUT: f32`. How long a toast stays when it names no timeout, in seconds: the source apps' notification duration.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::Toast`: `pub struct Toast { pub message: Label, pub timeout: Option<f32>, pub politeness: Politeness, pub tone: Tone, pub title: Option<Label>, pub actions: Vec<ToastAction> }`. A message the user sees for a while.
  - `pub fn new(message: impl Into<Label>) -> Self`. A neutral toast of `message` that stays [`TIMEOUT`] seconds and is announced politely.
  - `pub fn tone(mut self, tone: Tone) -> Self`. Sets the tone the card reads in.
  - `pub fn title(mut self, title: impl Into<Label>) -> Self`. Sets the title above the message.
  - `pub fn action(mut self, action: ToastAction) -> Self`. Adds an action after the message.
  - `pub fn timeout(mut self, timeout: Option<f32>) -> Self`. Sets how long the card stays.
  - `pub fn politeness(mut self, politeness: Politeness) -> Self`. Sets the announcement's politeness.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::ToastAction`: `pub struct ToastAction { pub label: Label }`. An action of a toast card: a button-like link that ends the toast and is reported by `end`.
  - `pub fn new(label: impl Into<Label>) -> Self`. An action named `label`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::ToastEnd`: `pub enum ToastEnd { Action(usize), Dismissed, Expired }`. How a toast left the queue.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::ToastId`: `pub struct ToastId`. A posted toast's handle.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::cards`: `pub fn cards(ctx: &Context) -> Vec<Card>`. The cards of the running viewport, oldest first.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::dismiss`: `pub fn dismiss(ctx: &Context, id: ToastId)`. Removes a toast before its timer ends, from whichever viewport holds it. The app asked for it, so no end is
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::end`: `pub fn end(ctx: &Context, id: ToastId) -> Option<ToastEnd>`. How the toast `id` ended, once: `None` while it is queued, after the report was read, and for a toast the cap
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::keep`: `pub fn keep(ctx: &Context, id: ToastId)`. Keeps the toast `id` until it is closed, whatever its timeout (an error the user must read).
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::post`: `pub fn post(ctx: &Context, toast: Toast) -> ToastId`. Posts `toast` to the running viewport.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::post_to`: `pub fn post_to(ctx: &Context, viewport: ViewportId, toast: Toast) -> ToastId`. Posts `toast` to `viewport`.
- `craft_ui::runtime::toast::shown`: `pub fn shown(ctx: &Context, id: ToastId, text: &str, held: bool)`. The host shows `id` in this pass; `held` says the pointer or focus is on it. The first show announces it.

  - `pub fn segment_control(&self) -> ControlMetrics`. `control`, with the side padding of a segment of a segmented control: `space.pad.control` at the small size

## `craft_ui::testing::access`

What the accessibility trials read: the AccessKit tree a frame produced, and the label scope a container opens.

- `craft_ui::testing::access::begin_label_scope`: `pub fn begin_label_scope(ctx: &Context, container: Id, label: FieldLabel)`. Opens the label scope of `container` with `label`, as a field container's body step does; the container's
- `craft_ui::testing::access::focused_key`: `pub fn focused_key(tree: &AccessTree) -> NodeKey`. The node AccessKit reports as focused.
- `craft_ui::testing::access::is_interactive`: `pub fn is_interactive(tree: &AccessTree, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether the node declares an action a user can take: focus, click or a value change.
- `craft_ui::testing::access::name`: `pub fn name(tree: &AccessTree, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The accessible name of the node, when it has a non-empty one: its own, else the first non-empty name of the nodes
- `craft_ui::testing::access::tree_of`: `pub fn tree_of(output: &mut crate::runtime::egui::fixture::FullOutput) -> AccessTree`. The AccessKit tree a finished pass of a viewport produced, taken out of its output; empty when it made none.
- `craft_ui::testing::access::violations`: `pub fn violations(ctx: &Context, tree: &AccessTree) -> Vec<String>`. The rule violations of a frame: interactive nodes with no name, and every finding the runtime's role rules
- `craft_ui::testing::access::AccessTree`: `pub struct AccessTree`. The AccessKit tree update of one frame.
  - `pub fn has(&self, id: Id) -> bool`. Whether the node of `id` exists.
  - `pub fn role(&self, id: Id) -> Option<Role>`. The role of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn name(&self, id: Id) -> Option<&str>`. The name of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn description(&self, id: Id) -> Option<&str>`. The description of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn description_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The description of a node by key.
  - `pub fn shortcut_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The keyboard shortcut a node by key lists.
  - `pub fn value_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The text value of a node by key.
  - `pub fn value(&self, id: Id) -> Option<&str>`. The text value of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn numeric(&self, id: Id) -> Option<Numeric>`. The numeric value and range of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn is_disabled(&self, id: Id) -> bool`. Whether the node of `id` is reported as disabled.
  - `pub fn declares(&self, id: Id, action: Declared) -> bool`. Whether the node of `id` declares `action`.
  - `pub fn labelled_by(&self, id: Id) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. The nodes that name the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn labelled_by_key(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. [`AccessTree::labelled_by`] for a node by key.
  - `pub fn described_by(&self, id: Id) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. The nodes that describe the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn position(&self, id: Id) -> Option<(usize, usize)>`. The place of the node of `id` in its set: its zero-based index and the size of the set.
  - `pub fn grid_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> GridPlace`. The row and column indexes and counts of a node by key.
  - `pub fn sort_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<bool>`. The sort order a column header node by key shows: `true` descending.
  - `pub fn declares_sort(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether a column header node declares a sort direction: it sorts when activated, sorted now or not.
  - `pub fn expanded(&self, id: Id) -> Option<bool>`. The expanded state of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn has_popup(&self, id: Id) -> Option<PopupKind>`. The kind of popup the node of `id` opens.
  - `pub fn controls(&self, id: Id) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. The nodes the node of `id` controls.
  - `pub fn is_modal(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether the node of `key` is modal.
  - `pub fn live(&self, id: Id) -> Option<Politeness>`. The live politeness of the node of `id`.
  - `pub fn invalid(&self, id: Id) -> bool`. Whether the node of `id` is marked invalid.
  - `pub fn run_texts(&self, id: Id) -> Vec<String>`. The text of each `TextRun` node below `id`, in order.
  - `pub fn text_selection(&self, id: Id) -> Option<((NodeKey, usize),(NodeKey, usize))>`. The selection of the text node `id` as the run node and character index of its anchor and focus.
  - `pub fn name_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<&str>`. The name of a node by key.
  - `pub fn toggled_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<craft_behavior::Mixed<bool>>`. The toggled state of a node by key.
  - `pub fn expanded_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<bool>`. The expanded state of a node by key.
  - `pub fn has_popup_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<PopupKind>`. The kind of popup the node of `key` opens.
  - `pub fn access_key_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<char>`. The access key of the node of `key`.
  - `pub fn current_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether the node of `key` is the current item of its set (the current page).
  - `pub fn parent_of(&self, id: Id) -> Option<NodeKey>`. The nearest ancestor of `id` that screen readers see: AccessKit's consumer drops generic containers.
  - `pub fn parent_of_key(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<NodeKey>`. [`AccessTree::parent_of`] for a node by key.
  - `pub fn in_ltr_axis(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether the node is, or lies inside, a component whose horizontal axis keeps its left-to-right order
  - `pub fn keys(&self) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. Every node that assistive technology reports.
  - `pub fn node_count(&self) -> usize`. The number of nodes that assistive technology reports.
  - `pub fn role_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<Role>`. The role of a node by key.
  - `pub fn selected_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<bool>`. Whether a node by key is reported as selected: `None` for a node that reports no selected state.
  - `pub fn bounds_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Option<egui::Rect>`. The bounds of a node by key, in points.
  - `pub fn is_disabled_key(&self, key: NodeKey) -> bool`. Whether a node by key is reported as disabled.
  - `pub fn declares_key(&self, key: NodeKey, action: Declared) -> bool`. Whether a node by key declares `action`.
  - `pub fn children_of(&self, key: NodeKey) -> Vec<NodeKey>`. The children of a node by key.
- `craft_ui::testing::access::Declared`: `pub enum Declared { Focus, Click, Increment, Decrement, SetValue, SetTextSelection, Expand, Collapse, Custom, ScrollUp, ScrollDown, ScrollLeft, ScrollRight }`. An action a node declares.
- `craft_ui::testing::access::NodeKey`: `pub struct NodeKey`. A node's number in the tree.
  - `pub fn of(id: Id) -> NodeKey`. The key of the node of `id`.

## `craft_ui::testing::contract`

What the contract trials read: the decision records and findings of the pipeline.

- `craft_ui::testing::contract::clicked_by_primary`: `pub fn clicked_by_primary(response: &egui::Response) -> bool`. Whether `response` reports a primary-button pointer click.
- `craft_ui::testing::contract::edit_response`: `pub fn edit_response<T>(response: egui::Response) -> crate::contract::EditResponse<T>`. An edit response with no events, for a trial component that has no `cx.edit_response()` to call.

- `craft_ui::testing::contract::raw_widget`: `pub fn raw_widget(ui: &mut egui::Ui, rect: egui::Rect)`. Places a clickable egui widget in `rect` by hand, the way a bypass of the components does.

- `craft_ui::testing::contract::take_reveal`: `pub fn take_reveal(ctx: &Context, owner: Id) -> Option<egui::Rect>`. The reveal request addressed to the scroll owner `owner`, once.

## `craft_ui::testing::dump`

Text dumps for tests: ordered `key = value` lines compared exactly with a file, so a change in

- `craft_ui::testing::dump::BUDGET`: `pub const BUDGET: usize`. The most lines the committed style dumps may hold in total.
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::Dump`: `pub struct Dump`. Ordered `key = value` lines.
  - `pub fn new() -> Self`. An empty dump.
  - `pub fn comment(&mut self, text: &str) -> &mut Self`. A `# text` line, e.g. naming the test that writes the file.
  - `pub fn line(&mut self, key: &str, value: impl DumpValue) -> &mut Self`. Appends `key = value`, with `key` under the current [`group`](Self::group) prefix.
  - `pub fn group(&mut self, prefix: &str, f: impl FnOnce(&mut Dump)) -> &mut Self`. Runs `f` with `prefix.` put before every key it writes.
  - `pub fn text(&self) -> &str`. The lines written so far.
  - `pub fn entries(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item =(String, &str)>`. The `(key, value)` pairs written so far, keys with their group prefixes; comments are
  - `pub fn compare(&self, path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<(), String>`. Compares with the file at `path`, or rewrites it when [`update_requested`]. The error
  - `pub fn check(&self, path: impl AsRef<Path>)`. [`compare`](Self::compare), failing the test on a mismatch.
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::DumpValue`: `pub trait DumpValue`. A value's text in a dump.
  - `fn dump_value(&self) -> String`. The value as dump text.
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::Entries`: `pub type Entries = Vec<(String, String)>`. The `(key, value)` lines of one theme, collected to be written with the other themes' (see [`Themed`]).
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::Themed`: `pub struct Themed`. Dump lines of one group in several themes, written as one line per key plus one per differing theme.
  - `pub fn new() -> Self`. No themes yet.
  - `pub fn theme(&mut self, name: &str, entries: Entries) -> &mut Self`. Adds the lines `entries` of theme `name`.
  - `pub fn write(&self, dump: &mut Dump)`. Writes each key once, in first-seen order, with its most frequent value (the first of equals), then
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::check_budget`: `pub fn check_budget(skip: &[&str])`. Fails when the dumps outside `skip` hold more than [`BUDGET`] lines.
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::check_dir`: `pub fn check_dir(dir: impl AsRef<Path>, dumps: &[(String, Dump)])`. Checks a directory one test owns: every `(file name, dump)` pair, and no other `.txt` files
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::diff`: `pub fn diff(old: &str, new: &str) -> String`. The lines only in `old` (`- `) and only in `new` (`+ `), at most 20. Dump keys are unique, so
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::dump_lines`: `pub fn dump_lines(skip: &[&str]) -> usize`. The lines of every dump file under `crates/*/tests/dumps` except those of the crates in `skip`, comments
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::dump_resolved`: `pub fn dump_resolved<T: std::fmt::Debug>(dump: &mut Dump, name: &str, resolve: impl Fn(&UiTokens) -> T)`. What `resolve` reads from the tokens of every theme of [`themes`], written into `dump` under `name`: each key
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::dump_style`: `pub fn dump_style<K: StyleKey, S: ComponentStyle + std::fmt::Debug>(dump: &mut Dump, ctx: &egui::Context, name: &str, variants: &[Variant], size: ControlSize, key: K, default: for<'a> fn(&StyleCx<'a>, K) -> S,)`. The resolved style of the component whose `style` function is `default`, for each of `variants` and in every
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::flatten_debug`: `pub fn flatten_debug(value: &dyn std::fmt::Debug) -> Entries`. The fields of a value as `(path, text)` pairs, read from its pretty `Debug` form: a struct field is its name, a
- `craft_ui::testing::dump::themes`: `pub fn themes() -> Vec<(String, UiTokens)>`. Every built-in theme in each mode it has, at standard contrast, as (`<theme>.<mode>`, tokens).

- `craft_ui::testing::focus::primary_button`: `pub fn primary_button(pos: Pos2, pressed: bool) -> Event`. A primary button press or release at `pos`.

  - `pub fn take_set_value(&mut self) -> Option<f64>`. Takes the AccessKit `SetValue` request aimed at the component's own node.

  - `pub fn take_access(&mut self) -> Option<AccessRequest>`. Takes the next `Intent::Access` request.

  - `pub fn text(mut self) -> Self`. Marks the stop as a text part.

## `craft_ui::testing::frames`

A fixed-rate frame driver over a context.

- `craft_ui::testing::frames::Frames`: `pub struct Frames { pub ctx: Context }`. One context, advanced frame by frame at 60 Hz.
  - `pub fn screen(&mut self, room: Vec2)`. The size of the egui screen rect in every following frame: `room` where it is larger than the default
  - `pub fn new() -> Self`. A fresh context with the runtime installed.
  - `pub fn pixels_per_point(&mut self, pixels_per_point: f32)`. The window's pixels per point in every following frame.
  - `pub fn record_paint(&mut self)`. Keeps what every following frame paints, for [`Frames::take_paint`].
  - `pub fn take_paint(&mut self) -> Option<fixture::FullOutput>`. What the frames painted since the last call, as a renderer takes it: the last frame's shapes and every
  - `pub fn uninstalled() -> Self`. A fresh context without the runtime, for frames that ran before `install`.
  - `pub fn install(&mut self)`. Installs the runtime on the context.
  - `pub fn host(&mut self, host: Arc<Host>)`. The host every following frame is given.
  - `pub fn root(&mut self, root: RootEnv)`. The root values every following frame is given.
  - `pub fn commands(&mut self, commands: CommandInput)`. The command input every following frame is given.
  - `pub fn frame(&mut self, events: Vec<Event>, mut ui: impl FnMut(&mut Ui)) -> usize`. One frame with `events`; `ui` runs once per pass. Returns the number of passes.
  - `pub fn frame_without_input(&mut self, events: Vec<Event>, ui: impl FnMut(&mut Ui)) -> bool`. One frame with no `set_input`, as an app that never calls it would drive it: the root values and the
  - `pub fn frame_in_viewport(&mut self, viewport: egui::ViewportId, ui: impl FnMut(&mut Ui))`. One pass with no events in `viewport`, a child window of the root, between two frames of the root.
  - `pub fn frame_shapes(&mut self, mut ui: impl FnMut(&mut Ui)) -> Vec<egui::Shape>`. One frame with no events; returns the shapes it painted, nested shapes flattened.
  - `pub fn hold(&mut self, modifiers: egui::Modifiers)`. The modifiers held in every following frame.
  - `pub fn hover_files(&mut self, names: &[&str])`. Files named `names` are dragged over the window from the OS, in this frame and every following one until they
  - `pub fn hover_typed_files(&mut self, files: &[(&str, &str)])`. Like `hover_files`, with the media type the OS reports for each file while it hovers.
  - `pub fn leave_files(&mut self)`. The hovering files leave the window without a drop.
  - `pub fn drop_files(&mut self, names: &[&str])`. The hovering files drop on the window in the next frame; `names` are what the OS reports as dropped.
  - `pub fn advance(&mut self, seconds: f64)`. Moves the clock on by `seconds` before the next frame.
  - `pub fn idle(&mut self, n: usize, mut ui: impl FnMut(&mut Ui))`. `n` frames with no events.
  - `pub fn ime(&self) -> Option<&fixture::ImeSeen>`. The IME output of the last frame: `None` when it wrote none.
  - `pub fn copied(&self) -> &[String]`. The texts the last frame put on the clipboard.
  - `pub fn texts(&self) -> &[fixture::PaintedText]`. The text runs the last frame painted.
  - `pub fn shape_bounds(&self) -> &[egui::Rect]`. The visual bounds of the shapes the last frame painted.
  - `pub fn shape_overshoot(&self) -> &[f32]`. Half the stroke width of the shape behind each of `shape_bounds`.
  - `pub fn tree(&mut self) -> AccessTree`. The AccessKit tree the last frame produced.
  - `pub fn repaint_delay(&self) -> Option<Duration>`. The wait the last frame asked for before the next pass: `Duration::ZERO` for an immediate repaint,
  - `pub fn repaint_requested(&self) -> bool`. Whether anything asked for a pass since the last frame ended.
  - `pub fn cursor_icon(&self) -> egui::CursorIcon`. The system cursor the last frame's output asked for.
- `craft_ui::testing::frames::announce_text`: `pub fn announce_text(ctx: &Context, text: &str)`. Adds `text` to the next frame output's announcements, as the runtime's access service does.
- `craft_ui::testing::frames::show_area`: `pub fn show_area<R>(ctx: &Context, name: &'static str, body: impl FnOnce(&mut Ui) -> R) -> R`. Shows an egui `Area` and runs `body` on its `Ui`.
- `craft_ui::testing::frames::widest_layer`: `pub fn widest_layer(ctx: &Context) -> f32`. The width of the widest layer above the background, 0 when there is none.

- `craft_ui::testing::layout::BlockStyle`: `pub struct BlockStyle`. The style of a `Block`: no padding.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::allocate`: `pub fn allocate(ui: &mut Ui, size: Vec2) -> egui::Rect`. Takes `size` in the layout of `ui` and returns its rect.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::body`: `pub fn body(ui: &mut Ui, key: &'static str, layout: &BodyLayout, sizes: &[Vec2]) -> Vec<Rect>`. Runs a body laid out by `layout` under `key` and places one child per entry of `sizes` in flow, as step 6
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::entry_id`: `pub fn entry_id(ui: &Ui, key: &'static str) -> Id`. The id a keyed entry of this `Ui` lives under.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::in_region`: `pub fn in_region<R>(ui: &mut Ui, rect: egui::Rect, body: impl FnOnce(&mut Ui) -> R) -> R`. Runs `body` in a child `Ui` whose region is `rect`.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::in_row_region`: `pub fn in_row_region<R>(ui: &mut Ui, rect: egui::Rect, body: impl FnOnce(&mut Ui) -> R) -> R`. Runs `body` in a child `Ui` whose region is `rect`, laid out as a row with its items centred across.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::is_animating`: `pub fn is_animating(ui: &Ui, key: &'static str) -> bool`. Whether the tween under `key` is still running.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::measured_block`: `pub fn measured_block(ui: &Ui, key: &'static str, real: Vec2) -> Option<Vec2>`. A block that placed itself from `key`'s entry of last pass and measured `real` this pass. Returns the size
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::peek_size`: `pub fn peek_size(ui: &Ui, key: &'static str) -> Option<Vec2>`. The size under `key` without a placement using it.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::read_size`: `pub fn read_size(ui: &Ui, key: &'static str) -> Option<Vec2>`. The size under `key` as a placement reads it.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::reordered_fonts`: `pub fn reordered_fonts() -> egui::FontDefinitions`. The test font definitions in another order.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::start_tween`: `pub fn start_tween(ui: &Ui, key: &'static str, from: Vec2, to: Vec2, duration: f64)`. Starts a size tween under `key`.
- `craft_ui::testing::layout::wrapped_text`: `pub fn wrapped_text(ui: &mut Ui, text: &str)`. Shows `text` wrapped to the available width of a sizing slot, for `measure_slot` trials.

## `craft_ui::testing::memory`

The memory services the trials check: a stage fixture that implements `Remember`, eviction, `forget` and

- `craft_ui::testing::memory::Stage`: `pub struct Stage`. A memory stage opened the way the pipeline opens one at step 3: the owner is registered, then the stage
  - `pub fn open(ctx: &Context, base: Id) -> Self`. A stage whose entries belong to `base`, which is registered as its own owner.
  - `pub fn open_owned(ctx: &Context, base: Id, owner: Id) -> Self`. A stage keyed by `base` whose entries belong to `owner`, registered here.
  - `pub fn open_peek(ctx: &Context, base: Id) -> Self`. The stage of a sizing pass: it reads kept values and writes nothing.
  - `pub fn finish(self)`. Writes every slot back to context data.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::dispose_evicted`: `pub fn dispose_evicted(ctx: &Context)`. Drops the slots the end hook evicted.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::facts_of_last_pass`: `pub fn facts_of_last_pass<T: Clone + Send + Sync + 'static>(ctx: &Context) -> Vec<T>`. The facts the running viewport's previous pass registered.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::forget`: `pub fn forget(ctx: &Context, id: Id)`. Removes the session slots under `id`.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::forget_saved`: `pub fn forget_saved(ctx: &Context, key: &SaveKey)`. Removes the saved slots of `key`.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::global`: `pub fn global(name: &'static str, scope: impl Hash + Debug) -> Id`. The id of a runtime singleton named `name` under `scope`.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::put`: `pub fn put<T: std::any::Any + Clone + Send + Sync>(ctx: &Context, id: Id, value: T)`. Stores `value` in context data under `id`, as a slot's `Drop` may.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::register_fact`: `pub fn register_fact<T: Clone + Send + Sync + 'static>(ctx: &Context, fact: T)`. Registers `fact` for the next pass of the running viewport.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::scoped`: `pub fn scoped<R>(ui: &mut Ui, f: impl FnOnce(&mut Ui) -> R) -> R`. Runs `f` on a child `Ui` of `ui`.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::scoped_by`: `pub fn scoped_by<R>(ui: &mut Ui, salt: impl std::hash::Hash + std::fmt::Debug, f: impl FnOnce(&mut Ui) -> R) -> R`. Runs `f` on a child `Ui` of `ui` with its own id scope named by `salt`.
- `craft_ui::testing::memory::sub`: `pub fn sub(base: Id, tag: impl Hash + Debug) -> Id`. The runtime sub-id of `base` for `tag`.

## `craft_ui::testing::pointer`

Pointer events and gestures for trials that drive frames themselves.

- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::cancel_captured_presses`: `pub fn cancel_captured_presses(ctx: &egui::Context)`. Asks the pointer service to end every captured press in the running pass, as Escape does. Call it from the
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::click`: `pub fn click(frames: &mut Frames, at: Pos2, show: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Ui))`. Hover in one frame, press in the next, release in the next, then one idle frame.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::drag`: `pub fn drag(frames: &mut Frames, from: Pos2, by: Vec2, show: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Ui))`. Press at `from`, move to `from + by` in four steps over four frames, release.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::gone`: `pub fn gone() -> Event`. The pointer leaving the window.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::middle`: `pub fn middle(pos: Pos2, pressed: bool) -> Event`. The middle button going down or up at `pos`.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::moved`: `pub fn moved(pos: Pos2) -> Event`. The pointer moving to `pos`.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::press`: `pub fn press(pos: Pos2) -> Event`. The primary button going down at `pos`.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::press_captured`: `pub fn press_captured(ctx: &egui::Context) -> bool`. Whether an open press holds the pointer in the running viewport, as Escape reads it.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::release`: `pub fn release(pos: Pos2) -> Event`. The primary button going up at `pos`.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::secondary`: `pub fn secondary(pos: Pos2, pressed: bool) -> Event`. The secondary button going down or up at `pos`.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::tap`: `pub fn tap(frames: &mut Frames, at: Pos2, gone_after: bool, show: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Ui))`. A touchpad tap: hover in one frame, then press and release inside the next. `gone` adds the pointer
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::wheel`: `pub fn wheel(dy: f32) -> Event`. A vertical wheel event of `dy` points.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::wheel_lines`: `pub fn wheel_lines(dy: f32) -> Event`. A mouse wheel turning `dy` lines at the pointer.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::window_blurred`: `pub fn window_blurred() -> Event`. The window losing focus.
- `craft_ui::testing::pointer::zoom_wheel`: `pub fn zoom_wheel(dy: f32) -> Event`. A vertical wheel event of `dy` points with Ctrl or Cmd held, which zooms.

## `craft_ui::testing::relations`

Relation assertions between rects and lengths: the tests compare layouts to each other and to tokens, never to

- `craft_ui::testing::relations::above`: `pub fn above(a: Rect, b: Rect) -> bool`. `a` ends above where `b` begins or earlier.
- `craft_ui::testing::relations::equals_token`: `pub fn equals_token(measured: f32, token: f32) -> bool`. `measured` equals the token value `token`.
- `craft_ui::testing::relations::grows_with`: `pub fn grows_with(base: f32, grown: f32) -> bool`. `grown` is at least `base` along one axis: a measure under a larger text size grows or stays.
- `craft_ui::testing::relations::inside`: `pub fn inside(inner: Rect, outer: Rect) -> bool`. `inner` lies within `outer`.
- `craft_ui::testing::relations::left_of`: `pub fn left_of(a: Rect, b: Rect) -> bool`. `a` ends where `b` begins or earlier.
- `craft_ui::testing::relations::mirrored`: `pub fn mirrored(left_to_right: Rect, right_to_left: Rect, within: Rect) -> bool`. `right_to_left` is `left_to_right` reflected across the vertical middle of `within`, with the same size and height.
- `craft_ui::testing::relations::same_height`: `pub fn same_height(a: Rect, b: Rect) -> bool`. `a` and `b` are equally tall.

## `craft_ui::testing::sensing`

Probe components for the sensing core: roots, members and parts described as data, shown through the same

- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::PartReport`: `pub struct PartReport { pub key: PartKey, pub index: Option<u64>, pub press: PressState, pub hovered: bool }`. What one part read this pass.
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::ProbeMember`: `pub struct ProbeMember`. A member of a probe arena, or a body child that is a root of its own in a probe that is no arena.
  - `pub fn new(name: &'static str, rect: Rect, sense: Sense) -> Self`. A member named `name` over `rect` sensing `sense`.
  - `pub fn part(mut self, part: ProbePart) -> Self`. Adds a part.
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::ProbePart`: `pub struct ProbePart`. A part of a probe.
  - `pub fn new(key: PartKey, rect: Rect, sense: Sense) -> Self`. A part `key` over `rect` sensing `sense`.
  - `pub fn level(mut self, level: HitLevel, sub: u8) -> Self`. The part's tier among the shapes of its stack position.
  - `pub fn over(mut self) -> Self`. The part sits above the body members.
  - `pub fn cursor(mut self, icon: CursorIcon) -> Self`. The cursor the part asks for while hovered.
  - `pub fn at(mut self, index: u64) -> Self`. One of a repeated part.
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::ProbeRoot`: `pub struct ProbeRoot`. A root: a leaf component, or with `arena` an arena root whose members join it.
  - `pub fn new(name: &'static str, rect: Rect, sense: Sense) -> Self`. A root named `name` over `rect` sensing `sense`.
  - `pub fn part(mut self, part: ProbePart) -> Self`. Adds an own part.
  - `pub fn member(mut self, member: ProbeMember) -> Self`. Adds a body member.
  - `pub fn arena(mut self) -> Self`. Makes the root an arena: its members join it and have no egui widget.
  - `pub fn transformed(mut self, transform: CanvasTransform) -> Self`. Shows the body through `transform` on its own egui layer (an arena only).
  - `pub fn blocker(mut self, size: Vec2) -> Self`. Adds an allocating egui widget of `size` over the body, as app code may.
  - `pub fn requests(mut self, cursor: impl Into<Cursor>, priority: CursorPriority) -> Self`. A cursor the root requests while the pointer is over it, as a tool does.
  - `pub fn disabled(mut self) -> Self`. Turns the root disabled.
  - `pub fn id(name: &'static str) -> Id`. The id of the root named `name`.
  - `pub fn transform_widget(name: &'static str) -> Id`. The id of a transformed root's forwarding widget.
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::RootReport`: `pub struct RootReport { pub root: SenseReport, pub activated_member: Option<Id>, pub members: Vec<SenseReport>, pub body: Option<TSTransform> }`. What a root and its members read this pass.
  - `pub fn to_screen(&self, local: Pos2) -> Pos2`. Where a point of the body appears on screen.
  - `pub fn member(&self, name: &'static str) -> Option<&SenseReport>`. The report of the member named `name`.
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::SenseReport`: `pub struct SenseReport { pub id: Id, pub press: PressState, pub parts: Vec<PartReport>, pub activated: bool, pub clicked: bool, pub hovered: bool }`. What a member, or a root's own shape and parts, read this pass.
  - `pub fn phase(&self, key: PartKey) -> PressPhase`. The press phase of the part `key`, `Idle` when the part is unknown.
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::cover`: `pub fn cover(ctx: &Context, size: Vec2)`. Shows an egui area of `size` over everything else, as a popup does.
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::egui_click_target`: `pub fn egui_click_target(ctx: &Context) -> Option<Id>`. The widget id egui's hit test chose as the click target at the latest pointer position.
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::press_focus`: `pub fn press_focus(ctx: &Context, at: Pos2) -> Option<PressFocus>`. How the focus service classifies a press at `at`, from last pass's shape record and egui's hit test at the
- `craft_ui::testing::sensing::show`: `pub fn show(ui: &mut Ui, roots: &[ProbeRoot]) -> Vec<RootReport>`. Shows `roots` in order on `ui`, each through the sensing core, and reads back what each saw.

The kind of a collection event, with the edit kind of the events that carry one.
